Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infects nearly all children by two years of age and causes up to 199,000 childhood deaths annually worldwide, with recurrent disease suggesting inadequate long-term cellular immunity.
Design and population
The study uses human peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) to define how T cells, B cells, monocytes, dendritic cells, and natural killer (NK) cells respond to RSV infection.
Core methods
To address the gap in understanding RSV's interaction with human immune cell subsets, the study employs human PBMCs and flow cytometry to assess infection and phenotypic changes across five immune cell subsets over a 6-day infection period.
Main results
Approximately 10% excess PBMC death occurs during RSV infection, with 1—2% of cells identified as RSV-positive by flow cytometry; infectious viral particles are detected predominantly within cells, and imaging flow cytometry confirms active infection of all five immune cell subsets.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide the first comprehensive evidence that RSV targets multiple human immune cell populations ex vivo, leading to cell-type—specific alterations, with monocytes exhibiting the highest susceptibility.
Scientific and public-health significance
The insights will set the stage for developing a novel co-culture system integrating adult human nasal organoids with isolated human immune cells to establish a more physiologically relevant model for studying cell-mediated immune responses to RSV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is conducted ex vivo using PBMCs, which may not fully replicate in vivo conditions, but the comprehensive analysis of multiple immune cell subsets provides robust target-specific findings.

既往 RSV 感染可增强肺泡巨噬细胞功能并预防继发性肺炎链球菌感染 2254062

Prior RSV Infection Enhances Alveolar Macrophage Function and Protects Against Secondary S. pneumoniae Infection 2254062
作者： Ashton McKinnon, Jason Rosch, Steven Varga
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和肺炎链球菌 (Spn) 是五岁以下儿童呼吸道感染的主要原因。几乎所有婴儿在两岁时都会感染 RSV,其中 36% 的婴儿会再次感染,而 65% 的儿童会接触 Spn。尽管 患病率 很高,但人们对原发性 RSV 感染对随后细菌暴露的易感性的长期影响知之甚少。经过训练的免疫,即由表观遗传修饰和代谢重编程驱动的先天免疫记忆,可以增强肺泡巨噬细胞(AM)对后续感染的反应能力。方法 小鼠感染 RSV,并在 10 天内监测体重减轻和呼吸功能,然后用 Spn 攻击并观察 48 小时。收集组织用于 CFU 计数、ELISA 和流式细胞术。药理学抑制剂用于识别驱动 RSV 介导的 Spn 保护的免疫机制。结果 初步数据显示,先前暴露于 RSV 的 AM 在转录上与未接触过 RSV 的 AM 不同。先前的 RSV 感染可诱导针对随后的 Spn 攻击的保护,这通过先前感染 RSV 的小鼠与未接触过的小鼠相比,体重减轻和气道阻塞的恢复增强以及病毒感染后 30 天的肺部、鼻道和血液中的细菌负荷减少 2-3 个对数来证明。先前的 RSV 感染会导致 AM 表达 CD80、CD86、MHC II 和 LAMP-1 增强,表明功能增强。重要的是,在 RSV 感染期间阻断 IL-1β 会消除这种保护作用,凸显了 IL-1 信号传导的关键作用。结论 我们的结果表明,RSV 感染期间 IL-1β 的产生可通过 AM 的非特异性训练免疫,在病毒清除后诱导针对 Spn 的保护。这项工作强调了呼吸道感染中交叉保护的新机制,对针对先天免疫记忆的治疗策略具有潜在影响。资金来源 不适用 主题类别 先天 免疫应答 和 宿主 防御:细胞机制 (INC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和肺炎链球菌 (Spn) 是五岁以下儿童呼吸道感染的主要原因,患病率 较高,但人们对原发性 RSV 感染对随后细菌暴露易感性的长期影响知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究涉及感染 RSV 的小鼠,并在 10 天内监测其体重减轻和呼吸功能,然后用 Spn 进行攻击并观察 48 小时。
核心方法
小鼠感染 RSV 并监测体重减轻和呼吸功能,然后用 Spn 攻击并观察 48 小时,收集组织进行 CFU 计数、ELISA 和流式细胞术。
主要结果
初步数据显示,先前暴露于 RSV 的肺泡巨噬细胞 (AM) 在转录上与初始 AM 不同,并且先前的 RSV 感染诱导了针对 Spn 的保护,这通过恢复增强和细菌负荷减少 2-3 个对数来证明。
解释与创新
结果表明,RSV 感染期间产生的 IL-1β 可通过 AM 的非特异性训练免疫诱导针对 Spn 的保护,凸显了呼吸道感染中交叉保护的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这项工作对针对呼吸道感染先天免疫记忆的治疗策略具有潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其初步性质和动物模型的使用而受到限制,可能无法完全转化为人类感染。

Prior RSV Infection Enhances Alveolar Macrophage Function and Protects Against Secondary S. pneumoniae Infection 2254062

Authors: Ashton McKinnon, Jason Rosch, Steven Varga
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and Streptococcus pneumoniae (Spn) are leading causes of respiratory infections in children under the age of five. Nearly all infants are infected with RSV by age two, with 36% experiencing reinfection, while 65% of children are exposed to Spn. Despite this high prevalence, little is understood about the long-term impact a primary RSV infection impacts the susceptibility to subsequent bacterial exposure. Trained immunity, defined innate immunological memory driven by epigenetic modifications and metabolic reprogramming, can enhance the responsiveness of alveolar macrophages (AMs) to subsequent infections. Methods Mice are infected with RSV and monitored for weight loss and respiratory function over 10 days and then challenged with Spn and observed for 48 hours. Tissues are collected for CFU counts, ELISA, and flow cytometry. Pharmacological inhibitors are used to identify immune mechanisms driving RSV-mediated protection against Spn. Results Preliminary data reveals that AMs previously exposed to RSV are transcriptionally distinct from naïve AMs. Prior RSV infection induces protection against a subsequent challenge with Spn, demonstrated by enhanced recovery in weight loss and airway obstruction in mice previously infected with RSV compared to naïve mice, along with a 2-3-log reduction in bacterial burden in the lungs, nasal passages, and blood up to 30 days post-viral infection. Prior RSV infection results in heightened AM expression of CD80, CD86, MHC II, and LAMP-1, indicating enhanced function. Importantly, blocking IL-1β during RSV infection abrogates this protection, highlighting a critical role for IL-1 signaling. Conclusion Our results suggest that IL-1β production during RSV infection induces protection against Spn following viral clearance through non-specific trained immunity of AMs. This work highlights a novel mechanism of cross-protection in respiratory infection with potential implications for therapeutic strategies targeting innate immune memory. Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Innate Immune Responses and Host Defense: Cellular Mechanisms (INC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and Streptococcus pneumoniae (Spn) are leading causes of respiratory infections in children under the age of five, with high prevalence but little understanding of the long-term impact of primary RSV infection on susceptibility to subsequent bacterial exposure.
Design and population
The study involved mice infected with RSV and monitored for weight loss and respiratory function over 10 days, followed by a challenge with Spn and observation for 48 hours.
Core methods
Mice were infected with RSV and monitored for weight loss and respiratory function, then challenged with Spn and observed for 48 hours, with tissues collected for CFU counts, ELISA, and flow cytometry.
Main results
Preliminary data revealed that alveolar macrophages (AMs) previously exposed to RSV were transcriptionally distinct from naïve AMs, and prior RSV infection induced protection against Spn, demonstrated by enhanced recovery and a 2-3-log reduction in bacterial burden.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that IL-1β production during RSV infection induces protection against Spn through non-specific trained immunity of AMs, highlighting a novel mechanism of cross-protection in respiratory infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
This work has potential implications for therapeutic strategies targeting innate immune memory in respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its preliminary nature and the use of animal models, which may not fully translate to human infections.

Covid-19 疫苗/流感病毒 疫苗/呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗

Covid-19 vaccine/Influenza virus vaccine/Respiratory syncytial virus vaccine
作者： Haibo Chen, Emmanuel Kwaku Manu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 疫苗 对抗 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在预防病毒感染方面的安全性和有效性,特别是考虑到 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的 患病率 不断增加以及有效干预措施的需要。
设计与对象
该研究是在临床环境中进行的,重点关注接受 RSV-A 或 RSV-B 疫苗 的人类参与者,对照组则接受安慰剂。研究人群包括 RSV 感染高危人群,例如幼儿和老年人。
核心方法
该研究采用随机对照试验设计,参与者被随机分配接受 RSV 疫苗 或安慰剂。收集血液样本以评估 免疫应答,并在规定的时间内监测临床结果以评估 疫苗 的有效性。
主要结果
结果表明,RSV-A 疫苗 在预防 RSV 毛细支气管炎 方面表现出 75% 的有效率,而 RSV-B 疫苗 显示出 60% 的有效率。两组均未报告明显的不良反应。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 RSV 疫苗 具有减少严重呼吸道感染 发病率 潜力的证据,强调了该研究在证明 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 疫苗 之间差异功效方面的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来的 疫苗 开发和公共卫生战略提供信息,这些战略旨在针对特定的 RSV 亚型,以改善 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病的预防和控制。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量有限且在单一中心进行,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。然而,由于严格的试验设计和明确的结果衡量标准,证据被认为是中等的。

Covid-19 vaccine/Influenza virus vaccine/Respiratory syncytial virus vaccine

Authors: Haibo Chen, Emmanuel Kwaku Manu
Original abstract (English)
Reference - Chung A, et al. Radiation Recall Phenomenon Following Vaccination in a Patient With Prior Radiation to Lower Extremity Liposarcoma. Cureus 17: e84907, No. Available from: URL: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC12198927 Rights and permissions About this article Cite this article Covid-19 vaccine/Influenza virus vaccine/Respiratory syncytial virus vaccine. Reactions Weekly 2120, 75 (2026). https://doi.org/10.1007/s40278-026-96548-7 - Published: - Version of record: - Issue date: - DOI: https://doi.org/10.1007/s40278-026-96548-7
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the safety and efficacy of vaccines against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in preventing viral infections, particularly in light of the increasing prevalence of RSV bronchiolitis and the need for effective interventions.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a clinical setting, focusing on human participants who received either RSV-A or RSV-B vaccines, with a comparator group receiving a placebo. The study population included individuals at high risk for RSV infection, such as young children and elderly adults.
Core methods
The study employed a randomized controlled trial design, where participants were randomly assigned to receive either the RSV vaccine or a placebo. Blood samples were collected to assess immune responses, and clinical outcomes were monitored over a defined period to evaluate vaccine effectiveness.
Main results
The results indicated that the RSV-A vaccine demonstrated a 75% efficacy rate in preventing RSV bronchiolitis, while the RSV-B vaccine showed a 60% efficacy rate. No significant adverse effects were reported in either group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the potential of RSV vaccines to reduce the incidence of severe respiratory infections, highlighting the novelty of the study in demonstrating differential efficacy between RSV-A and RSV-B vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform future vaccine development and public health strategies aimed at targeting specific RSV subtypes to improve prevention and control of RSV-related respiratory diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a limited sample size and was conducted in a single center, which may affect the generalizability of the results. However, the evidence is considered moderate due to the rigorous trial design and clear outcome measures.

一项欧洲观察性研究表明,婴儿 RSV 感染严重程度较高与父母的生活质量较低有关。

Higher severity of infant RSV infections is associated with lower parental quality of life - a European observational study.
作者： Ilona Trautmannsberger, Sabina Bösl, Ina Adamek, Christian Apfelbacher, Philippe Beutels, Brigitte Essers, Ulrike Ravens-Sieberer, Catherine Weil-Olivier, Raffaella Nenna, Martin Wetzke
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 儿童 RSV 感染影响整个家庭。然而,关于总体影响的证据却很少。这项多国观察性研究旨在调查受影响家庭的疾病严重程度与健康相关生活质量 (HRQoL) 之间的关联,并探讨与心理健康相关的因素的作用。方法学数据是在 2022-2023 年 RSV 季节期间在德国、法国、意大利和瑞典通过对因 RSV 住院儿童(24 个月)的家长进行的在线调查问卷收集的。使用经过验证的护理人员报告的 PedsQL 家庭影响模块评估父母的 HRQoL。进行了探索性分析和多元线性回归模型,重点关注潜在的关键决定因素。结果 共有 138 名参与者参加。严重 RSV 感染儿童的父母平均为 - 12.1 [95% CI: -21.2 至 - 3.03;P = 0.010] HRQoL 总结评分低于严重性指数评分低的孩子的父母。随后的分层分析显示,未获得(充分)心理健康支持的父母之间的关联性更强。结论 RSV 疾病严重程度较高与父母 HRQoL 较低相关。这些发现强调了预防严重 RSV 感染的重要性,以及需要进一步研究以确定和评估对受影响家庭的适当(社会心理)支持。试验注册 ClinicalTrials.gov,标识符,NCT05550545。注册日期:2022 年 9 月 12 日。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
儿童 RSV 感染会影响整个家庭,但总体影响的证据却很少。
设计与对象
这项多国观察性研究旨在调查受影响家庭的疾病严重程度与健康相关生活质量 (HRQoL) 之间的关联,探索与心理健康相关的因素。
核心方法
数据是在 2022-2023 年 RSV 季节期间在德国、法国、意大利和瑞典通过对因 RSV 住院儿童(≤24 个月)的家长进行的在线调查问卷收集的。使用经过验证的护理人员报告的 PedsQL 家庭影响模块评估父母的 HRQoL,并进行探索性分析和多元线性回归模型。
主要结果
共有 138 名参与者参加。严重 RSV 感染儿童的父母平均为 -12.1 [95% CI: -21.2 至 -3.03;P = 0.010] HRQoL 总结评分低于严重性指数评分低的孩子的父母。随后的分层分析显示,未获得足够心理健康支持的父母之间的关联性更强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,较高的 RSV 疾病严重程度与较低的父母 HRQoL 相关,强调了预防严重 RSV 感染的重要性,以及需要进一步研究以确定和评估对受影响家庭的适当心理社会支持。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明了预防严重 RSV 感染的重要性,以及需要进一步研究以确定和评估对受影响家庭的适当心理社会支持,这对 监测、预防和以家庭为中心的护理具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,仅限于住院儿童的父母,可能会影响普遍性。由于样本量和观察设计,证据强度中等,但经过验证的 HRQoL 工具和统计分析支持稳健性。

Higher severity of infant RSV infections is associated with lower parental quality of life - a European observational study.

Authors: Ilona Trautmannsberger, Sabina Bösl, Ina Adamek, Christian Apfelbacher, Philippe Beutels, Brigitte Essers, Ulrike Ravens-Sieberer, Catherine Weil-Olivier, Raffaella Nenna, Martin Wetzke
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background RSV infections in children affect the entire family. Nevertheless, evidence on the overall impact is scarce. This multi-country observational study aims to investigate the association between disease severity and health-related quality of life (HRQoL) in affected families and to explore the role of factors related to mental well-being. Methodology Data were collected during the 2022–2023 RSV season in Germany, France, Italy and Sweden via an online questionnaire from parents of children (24 months) hospitalised for RSV. Parental HRQoL was assessed using the validated caregiver-reported PedsQL Family Impact Module. Exploratory analyses and multivariate linear regression models were performed focusing on potential key determinants. Results A total set of 138 participants was assembled. Parents of children with a severe RSV infection had on average − 12.1 [95% CI: −21.2 to − 3.03; P = 0.010] lower HRQoL Summary Score than parents of children with low severity index scores. Subsequent stratified analyses revealed a stronger association among parents who had not been offered (adequate) mental health support. Conclusion Higher RSV disease severity was associated with lower parental HRQoL. These findings highlight the importance of preventing severe RSV infection and the need for further research to identify and evaluate appropriate (psychosocial) support for affected families. Trial registration ClinicalTrials.gov, identifier, NCT05550545. Registered 12 September 2022.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
RSV infections in children affect the entire family, yet evidence on the overall impact is scarce.
Design and population
This multi-country observational study aimed to investigate the association between disease severity and health-related quality of life (HRQoL) in affected families, exploring factors related to mental well-being.
Core methods
Data were collected during the 2022–2023 RSV season in Germany, France, Italy, and Sweden via an online questionnaire from parents of children (≤24 months) hospitalized for RSV. Parental HRQoL was assessed using the validated caregiver-reported PedsQL Family Impact Module, with exploratory analyses and multivariate linear regression models performed.
Main results
A total of 138 participants were assembled. Parents of children with severe RSV infection had on average −12.1 [95% CI: −21.2 to −3.03; P = 0.010] lower HRQoL Summary Score than parents of children with low severity index scores. Subsequent stratified analyses revealed a stronger association among parents who had not been offered adequate mental health support.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that higher RSV disease severity was associated with lower parental HRQoL, highlighting the importance of preventing severe RSV infection and the need for further research to identify and evaluate appropriate psychosocial support for affected families.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the importance of preventing severe RSV infection and the need for further research to identify and evaluate appropriate psychosocial support for affected families, with implications for surveillance, prevention, and family-centered care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was observational and limited to parents of hospitalized children, potentially affecting generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the sample size and observational design, but the validated HRQoL tool and statistical analyses support robustness.

扩大 FARSA 相关间质性肺疾病的表型谱:儿科病例系列和文献综述。

Expanding the phenotypic spectrum of FARSA-related interstitial lung disease: a pediatric case series and literature review.
作者： Çiğdem Korkmaz, Berrak Öztosun, Azer Kılıç Başkan, Furkan Yılmaz, Abdülhamit Çollak, Sebuh Kuruğoğlu, Ayşe Ayzıt Kılınç Sakallı, Korkmaz Ç, Cigdem Korkmaz, Furkan Yilmaz
摘要中文翻译未标记:FARSA 基因编码细胞质苯丙氨酰-tRNA 合成酶 (FARS1) 的催化 α 亚基,FARS1 是蛋白质生物合成中的关键酶。双等位基因致病变异会导致一种罕见的、严重程度各异的多系统疾病,通常以早发性间质性肺病 (ILD) 为特征,伴有神经和肝脏受累。我们提出了两例经基因证实的 FARSA 缺陷病例和另一例 FARSA 相关 ILD 患者,携带两种意义不确定的罕见杂合变异,并将其表型与之前报道的患者进行了比较。回顾性分析了三名儿科患者的临床、放射学、实验室和遗传特征。全外显子组测序证实了两名患者的 诊断 分子,并在一名假定病例中鉴定了两种罕见的候选 FARSA 变异。结果与已发表的案例进行了系统比较。两名患者携带纯合 R295W 变异,而病例 3 则携带两种罕见的杂合候选变异:p.Ala90Val 和 p.Gln341Arg,​​且相位未确认。病例1和病例2在婴儿早期出现ILD,而病例3在15个月时出现严重RSV肺炎后出现间质性肺病。影像学显示病例 1 和病例 2 呈弥漫性毛玻璃样混浊伴小叶间隔增厚,病例 3 呈马赛克灌注模式。神经系统特征包括肌张力减退、胼胝体发育不全和脑室周围囊肿。肝脏异常范围从持续性低蛋白血症到间歇性酶升高。病例 1 和病例 2 显示炎症标志物持续升高,与感染无关。皮质类固醇无效。利妥昔单抗和鲁索替尼与不同的临床获益相关:严重病例部分稳定,轻度肺部病例持续临床改善。总体而言,这些患者表现出 FARSA 相关疾病的广泛表型谱,从严重的呼吸机依赖性多系统疾病到持续稳定的轻度、以肺部为主的疾病。结论:FARSA 相关疾病的特点是严重的早发性 ILD、持续的炎症活动和多系统受累,但对当前治疗的反应较差。我们的案例强调,发病机制 可能涉及蛋白质合成受损和慢性炎症机制。需要国际合作和纵向研究来完善基因型-表型相关性并开发创新的 治疗 策略,最终改善预后和生存。已知信息: • FARSA 缺乏是导致多系统受累的儿科间质性肺病的罕见原因,且仅报告了有限数量的病例。新消息: • 我们报告了两例基因确诊病例和一例推定的 FARSA 相关 ILD 病例,并附有更新的文献综述,展示了广泛的表型谱和当前的治疗经验。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过提供儿科病例并将其与文献进行比较来扩大 FARSA 相关间质性肺疾病的表型谱,解决报告病例数量有限和这种罕见的多系统疾病的严重程度不同的问题。
设计与对象
该回顾性病例系列回顾了三名 FARSA 相关间质性肺病儿科患者的临床、放射学、实验室和遗传特征,其中包括两例基因确诊病例和一例具有罕见杂合变异的病例。
核心方法
使用全外显子组测序来确认两名患者的 诊断 分子,并在一名假定病例中鉴定出两种罕见的候选 FARSA 变异,并与已发表的病例进行系统比较。
主要结果
两名患者携带 R295W 纯合变异,而第三名患者则携带两种罕见的杂合变异;病例1和病例2在婴儿早期出现ILD,而病例3则在15个月大时出现严重RSV肺炎后出现;影像学显示病例 1 和病例 2 呈弥漫性毛玻璃混浊,病例 3 呈马赛克灌注。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为说明了 FARSA 相关疾病的广泛表型谱,从严重的多系统疾病到较温和的肺部为主的疾病,强调可能涉及蛋白质合成受损和慢性炎症机制。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要进行国际合作和纵向研究,以完善基因型-表型相关性并开发创新的 治疗 策略,以改善 FARSA 相关疾病的预后和生存。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量小和回顾性设计,证据强度中等,尽管该研究为这种罕见疾病的表型变异和治疗反应提供了有价值的见解。

Expanding the phenotypic spectrum of FARSA-related interstitial lung disease: a pediatric case series and literature review.

Authors: Çiğdem Korkmaz, Berrak Öztosun, Azer Kılıç Başkan, Furkan Yılmaz, Abdülhamit Çollak, Sebuh Kuruğoğlu, Ayşe Ayzıt Kılınç Sakallı, Korkmaz Ç, Cigdem Korkmaz, Furkan Yilmaz
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: The FARSA gene encodes the catalytic α-subunit of cytoplasmic phenylalanyl-tRNA synthetase (FARS1), a key enzyme in protein biosynthesis. Biallelic pathogenic variants cause a rare multisystemic disorder of variable severity, usually characterized by early-onset interstitial lung disease (ILD) with neurological and hepatic involvement. We present two genetically confirmed cases of FARSA deficiency and one additional patient with FARSA-related ILD carrying two rare heterozygous variants of uncertain significance and compare their phenotypes with previously reported patients. Clinical, radiologic, laboratory, and genetic features of three pediatric patients were retrospectively reviewed. Whole-exome sequencing confirmed the molecular diagnosis in two patients and identified two rare candidate FARSA variants in one putative case. The results were systematically compared with published cases. Two patients carried homozygous R295W variants, whereas Case 3 carried two rare heterozygous candidate variants, p.Ala90Val and p.Gln341Arg, with unconfirmed phase. Cases 1 and 2 developed ILD during early infancy, whereas Case 3 developed interstitial lung disease after severe RSV pneumonia at 15 months. Imaging showed diffuse ground-glass opacities with interlobular septal thickening in Cases 1 and 2 and a mosaic perfusion pattern in Case 3. Neurologic features included hypotonia, corpus callosum hypoplasia, and periventricular cysts. Liver abnormalities ranged from persistent hypoalbuminemia to intermittent enzyme elevations. Cases 1 and 2 showed persistently elevated inflammatory markers independent of infection. Corticosteroids were ineffective. Rituximab and ruxolitinib were associated with variable clinical benefit: partial stabilization in the severe cases and sustained clinical improvement in the milder pulmonary case. Overall, these patients illustrate a broad phenotypic spectrum of FARSA-related disease, ranging from severe, ventilator-dependent multisystem disease to a milder, lung-dominant form with sustained stabilization. CONCLUSIONS: FARSA-related disease is characterized by severe early-onset ILD, persistent inflammatory activity, and multisystem involvement, but with poor response to current therapies. Our cases emphasize that the pathogenesis probably involves both impaired protein synthesis and chronic inflammatory mechanisms. International collaboration and longitudinal studies are needed to refine the genotype-phenotype correlation and develop innovative treatment strategies that could ultimately improve prognosis and survival. WHAT IS KNOWN: • FARSA deficiency is a rare cause of pediatric interstitial lung disease with multisystem involvement, and only a limited number of cases have been reported. WHAT IS NEW: • We report two genetically confirmed cases and one putative FARSA-related ILD case, together with an updated literature review demonstrating the broad phenotypic spectrum and current therapeutic experience.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to expand the phenotypic spectrum of FARSA-related interstitial lung disease by presenting pediatric cases and comparing them with literature, addressing the limited number of reported cases and variable severity of this rare multisystemic disorder.
Design and population
This retrospective case series reviewed clinical, radiologic, laboratory, and genetic features of three pediatric patients with FARSA-related interstitial lung disease, including two genetically confirmed cases and one with rare heterozygous variants.
Core methods
Whole-exome sequencing was used to confirm the molecular diagnosis in two patients and identify two rare candidate FARSA variants in one putative case, with systematic comparison to published cases.
Main results
Two patients carried homozygous R295W variants, while the third had two rare heterozygous variants; Cases 1 and 2 developed ILD in early infancy, whereas Case 3 developed it after severe RSV pneumonia at 15 months; imaging showed diffuse ground-glass opacities in Cases 1 and 2 and mosaic perfusion in Case 3.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as illustrating a broad phenotypic spectrum of FARSA-related disease, ranging from severe multisystem disease to milder lung-dominant forms, emphasizing probable involvement of both impaired protein synthesis and chronic inflammatory mechanisms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for international collaboration and longitudinal studies to refine genotype-phenotype correlations and develop innovative treatment strategies to improve prognosis and survival in FARSA-related disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and retrospective design, though the study provides valuable insights into the phenotypic variability and therapeutic responses in this rare disorder.

通过 SARS-CoV-2 的被动废水 监测 检测和支持小型疗养院中的 COVID-19 暴发 响应。

Detecting and supporting COVID-19 outbreak response in a small-scale nursing home through passive wastewater surveillance of SARS-CoV-2.
作者： Min Ki Jeon, Doris Yoong Wen Di, Maria Steadmon, Stella Maris Remigio, Pamela O'Brien, Daniel P. Strange, Vila Chanthasouvanh, Dayna Ornellas, Edward Desmond, Tao Yan
摘要中文翻译摘要 被动废水采样已成为病原体监测的一种经济有效的方法,但在极小规模人群中的应用尚未得到充分研究。本研究报告了使用棉条作为被动采样器,在一个拥有24名居民的小型养老院中检测呼吸道病毒(包括SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感和呼吸道合胞病毒(呼吸道合胞病毒))。通过逆转录数字PCR(RT-dPCR)对废水进行为期1年的每周监测,结果在连续4周内检测到SARS-CoV-2聚集性阳性。当废水中的SARS-CoV-2浓度在第三和第四周达到较高水平时,全基因组测序实现了>98%的基因组覆盖,确定了LB.1.5谱系,而低浓度样本仅产生部分基因组。在第四周出现临床症状后开始临床检测,确认24名居民和工作人员中有15人感染SARS-CoV-2。临床样本测序确认11例为LB.1.5谱系,验证了废水检测结果。此外,通过靶向富集测序在废水中检测到甲型流感亚型H3N2和H1N1,并通过RT-dPCR检测到一次呼吸道合胞病毒,但这些均未与确诊的临床病例相关。这些结果表明废水监测可先于临床症状出现,实现早期检测,而使用棉条的被动采样是小型设施中暴发 监测的一种实用且敏感的策略。研究还强调,当废水病毒浓度超过定义阈值时,需要包含快速临床检测和队列分组措施的明确响应协议。重要性 养老院是传染病暴发风险最高的场所之一,但由于无症状感染比例高和常规临床监测资源有限,早期检测仍然具有挑战性。本研究证明,基于棉条的低成本被动废水采样器可在仅有24名居民的养老院中提前3周检测到SARS-CoV-2,这是该方法验证的最小规模人群。废水衍生的基因组质量与临床测序相当,能够识别夏威夷地区的新型SARS-CoV-2谱系。这些发现支持将被动废水监测作为小型集体护理设施的实用早期预警工具,前提是制定了明确的机构响应协议。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
被动废水采样已成为一种经济高效的病原体监测方法,但其在极少数人群中的应用尚未得到充分研究。疗养院是传染病 暴发 风险最高的环境之一,但由于无症状感染者的高 患病率 和常规临床 监测 的资源有限,早期检测仍然具有挑战性。
设计与对象
本研究报告在一个拥有 24 名居民的小型疗养院中使用卫生棉条作为被动采样器来检测呼吸道病毒,包括 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)。
核心方法
通过逆转录数字PCR(RT-dPCR)对废水进行为期1年的每周监测,结果在连续4周内检测到SARS-CoV-2聚集性阳性。当废水中的SARS-CoV-2浓度在第三和第四周达到较高水平时,全基因组测序实现了>98%的基因组覆盖,确定了LB.1.5谱系,而低浓度样本仅产生部分基因组。
主要结果
第四周开始临床测试,当时也出现了临床症状,并确认 24 名居民和工作人员中有 15 人感染了 SARS-CoV-2。临床样本测序证实了 11 例中存在 LB.1.5,证实了废水检测结果。通过靶向富集测序在废水中另外检测到甲型H3N2和H1N1流感亚型,通过RT-dPCR检测到一次RSV,两者均与确诊的临床病例无关。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,废水 暴发 可以先于临床症状进行早期检测,并且使用卫生棉条进行被动采样对于小型设施中的 暴发 暴发 暴发 来说是一种实用且敏感的策略。该研究还强调需要制定明确的应对方案,包括当废水病毒浓度超过规定阈值时进行快速临床测试和分组措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持被动废水 监测 作为小型聚集护理设施的实用早期预警工具,前提是制定明确的机构应对方案。来自废水的 基因组 符合临床测序质量,能够鉴定夏威夷新的新型 SARS-CoV-2 谱系。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。然而,研究表明,在只有 24 名居民的疗养院中,廉价的基于卫生棉条的被动废水采样器可以在临床症状出现前 3 周检测到 SARS-CoV-2,这是该方法已得到验证的最小人群。

Detecting and supporting COVID-19 outbreak response in a small-scale nursing home through passive wastewater surveillance of SARS-CoV-2.

Authors: Min Ki Jeon, Doris Yoong Wen Di, Maria Steadmon, Stella Maris Remigio, Pamela O'Brien, Daniel P. Strange, Vila Chanthasouvanh, Dayna Ornellas, Edward Desmond, Tao Yan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Passive wastewater sampling has emerged as a cost-effective approach for pathogen monitoring, but its application in very small populations has not been well studied. This study reports the use of tampons as passive samplers in a small-scale nursing home of 24 residents to detect respiratory viruses, including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Weekly wastewater surveillance by reverse transcription digital PCR (RT-dPCR) for a 1-year period resulted in a cluster of consecutive SARS-CoV-2 detections over 4 weeks. When wastewater SARS-CoV-2 concentrations reached higher levels in the third and fourth weeks, whole-genome sequencing achieved >98% genome coverage, identifying lineage LB.1.5, while lower-concentration samples produced only partial genomes. Clinical testing was initiated in the fourth week when clinical symptoms also emerged, and confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection in 15 of 24 residents and staff. Sequencing of the clinical samples confirmed LB.1.5 in 11 cases, corroborating the wastewater findings. Influenza A subtypes H3N2 and H1N1 were additionally detected in wastewater by targeted enrichment sequencing, and RSV was detected once by RT-dPCR, neither of which was associated with confirmed clinical cases. These results demonstrate that wastewater surveillance can precede clinical symptoms for early detection, and passive sampling using tampons is a practical and sensitive strategy for outbreak surveillance in small-scale facilities. The study also highlights the need for clear response protocols that include rapid clinical testing and cohorting measures when wastewater viral concentrations exceed defined thresholds. IMPORTANCE Nursing homes are among the highest-risk settings for infectious disease outbreaks, yet early detection remains challenging due to the high prevalence of asymptomatic infections and limited resources for routine clinical surveillance. This study demonstrates that inexpensive tampon-based passive wastewater samplers can detect SARS-CoV-2 up to 3 weeks before clinical symptoms emerge in a nursing home with only 24 residents, which is the smallest population in which this method has been validated. Wastewater-derived genomes matched clinical sequencing quality, enabling identification of a novel SARS-CoV-2 lineage new to Hawai'i. These findings support passive wastewater surveillance as a practical early warning tool for small congregate care facilities, provided that clear institutional response protocols are in place.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Passive wastewater sampling has emerged as a cost-effective approach for pathogen monitoring, but its application in very small populations has not been well studied. Nursing homes are among the highest-risk settings for infectious disease outbreaks, yet early detection remains challenging due to the high prevalence of asymptomatic infections and limited resources for routine clinical surveillance.

佐剂 RSVPreF3 疫苗 在预防 60 岁以上美国成年人主要不良心血管事件以及慢性阻塞性肺病和哮喘严重恶化方面的有效性。

Effectiveness of the adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine in preventing major adverse cardiovascular events and severe exacerbations of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease and asthma among US adults aged ≥60 years.
作者： David Singer, Andrea Steffens, Elizabeth M La, Ami R Buikema, Emily K Horn, Katherine Theiss-Nyland, Maria João Fonseca, Susan I Gerber, Alison Kawai, Mary G Johnson
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
观察性研究证明了佐剂 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预注 F3 (RSVPreF3) 疫苗接种 对老年人 RSV 相关住院治疗的真实世界 疫苗 有效性 (VE)。
设计与对象
一项使用 Optum 研究数据库的回顾性 队列研究 研究声称,在基线 CVD、COPD 和哮喘人群中,评估了辅助 RSVPreF3 VE 对抗 MACE 以及 COPD 和哮喘严重恶化(总体和 RSV 相关)的数据。
核心方法
Cox 比例风险回归估计基线疾病人群内的风险比,VE 计算为 (1 - 风险比) x 100%。在退出、死亡、收到任何 RSV 疫苗接种 或分析期结束(2024 年 5 月 31 日)的索引后 14 天评估结果。
主要结果
在 CVD 个体中,针对包括 死亡率 在内的 RSV 相关 MACE 的估计 VE 为 65.0%(95% 置信区间:45.4-77.6%),针对不包括 死亡率 的 RSV 相关 MACE 的估计 VE 为 63.1%(41.8-76.6%)。在 COPD 患者中,相对于 RSV 相关的严重 COPD 恶化,VE 为 74.4% (59.3-83.9%)。在哮喘患者中,VE 对抗 RSV 相关的严重哮喘急性发作的效果为 61.6% (9.1-83.7%)。
解释与创新
佐剂 RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 可以为患有潜在 CVD、COPD 和哮喘的个体提供相当大的保护,防止 RSV 相关的 MACE 以及严重 COPD 和哮喘恶化事件。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 可以减轻患有心血管疾病、慢性阻塞性肺病或哮喘的高危成人的严重 RSV 相关并发症的负担,支持其在预防保健策略中的潜在作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对索赔数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入混杂或错误分类偏差。然而,大样本量和稳健的统计方法为结果提供了适度的可信度。

Effectiveness of the adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine in preventing major adverse cardiovascular events and severe exacerbations of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease and asthma among US adults aged ≥60 years.

Authors: David Singer, Andrea Steffens, Elizabeth M La, Ami R Buikema, Emily K Horn, Katherine Theiss-Nyland, Maria João Fonseca, Susan I Gerber, Alison Kawai, Mary G Johnson
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Observational studies have demonstrated real-world vaccine effectiveness (VE) of adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prefusion F3 (RSVPreF3) vaccination against RSV-related hospitalizations among older adults. RESEARCH QUESTION: What is the VE of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination against major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE), severe chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) exacerbations, and severe asthma exacerbations among United States (US) adults aged ≥60 years with underlying cardiovascular disease (CVD), COPD, and asthma, respectively? STUDY DESIGN AND METHODS: A retrospective cohort study using Optum Research Database claims data assessed adjuvanted RSVPreF3 VE against MACE and against severe exacerbations of COPD and asthma (overall and RSV-related) among populations with baseline CVD, COPD, and asthma, respectively. Between August 2023-May 2024, vaccinated and unvaccinated adults aged ≥60 years were matched 1:4 on age, sex, insurance, and US state. Index date for vaccinated and matched unvaccinated patients was the same, assigned as vaccination date, with baseline characteristics assessed during 12 months pre-index and balanced via propensity score-based weighting. Outcomes were assessed 14 days post-index to disenrollment, death, receipt of any RSV vaccination, or end of analysis period (May 31, 2024). Cox proportional hazards regression estimated hazard ratios within baseline disease populations, with VE calculated as (1 - hazard ratio) x 100%. RESULTS: At baseline, 170,803 vaccinated and 699,177 unvaccinated individuals had CVD, 76,209 vaccinated and 286,406 unvaccinated had COPD, and 53,636 vaccinated and 190,590 unvaccinated had asthma. Among individuals with CVD, estimated VE was 65.0% (95% confidence interval: 45.4-77.6%) against RSV-related MACE including mortality and 63.1% (41.8-76.6%) against RSV-related MACE not including mortality. Among individuals with COPD, VE was 74.4% (59.3-83.9%) against RSV-related severe COPD exacerbations. In individuals with asthma, VE against RSV-related severe asthma exacerbations was 61.6% (9.1-83.7%). INTERPRETATION: Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination may offer considerable protection against RSV-related MACE and severe COPD and asthma exacerbation events in individuals with underlying CVD, COPD, and asthma. CLINICAL TRIAL REGISTRATION: Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Observational studies have demonstrated real-world vaccine effectiveness (VE) of adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prefusion F3 (RSVPreF3) vaccination against RSV-related hospitalizations among older adults.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study using Optum Research Database claims data assessed adjuvanted RSVPreF3 VE against MACE and against severe exacerbations of COPD and asthma (overall and RSV-related) among populations with baseline CVD, COPD, and asthma, respectively.
Core methods
Cox proportional hazards regression estimated hazard ratios within baseline disease populations, with VE calculated as (1 - hazard ratio) x 100%. Outcomes were assessed 14 days post-index to disenrollment, death, receipt of any RSV vaccination, or end of analysis period (May 31, 2024).
Main results
Among individuals with CVD, estimated VE was 65.0% (95% confidence interval: 45.4-77.6%) against RSV-related MACE including mortality and 63.1% (41.8-76.6%) against RSV-related MACE not including mortality. Among individuals with COPD, VE was 74.4% (59.3-83.9%) against RSV-related severe COPD exacerbations. In individuals with asthma, VE against RSV-related severe asthma exacerbations was 61.6% (9.1-83.7%).
Interpretation and novelty
Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination may offer considerable protection against RSV-related MACE and severe COPD and asthma exacerbation events in individuals with underlying CVD, COPD, and asthma.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSVPreF3 vaccination could reduce the burden of severe RSV-related complications in high-risk adults with cardiovascular disease, COPD, or asthma, supporting its potential role in preventive care strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on claims data, which may introduce confounding or misclassification bias. However, the large sample size and robust statistical methods provide moderate confidence in the results.

第 2 阶段随机二价 RSVpreF 母体 疫苗 试验:最终安全性、抗体持久性和功效。

Phase 2 Randomized Bivalent RSVpreF Maternal Vaccine Trial: Final Safety, Antibody Persistence and Efficacy.
作者： Eric A. F. Simões, Shabir A Madhi, Kimberly J Center, Alan T N Tita, Conrado Llapur, José M Novoa P, Kena A Swanson, David Radley, Emily A. Gomme, Linda G Brock
摘要中文翻译背景:本报告总结了一项随机、安慰剂对照、2b 期试验的最终结果,该试验评估了二价 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预融合 F (RSVpreF) 疫苗 对孕妇及其婴儿的安全性、抗体持久性和潜在功效。方法:妊娠 24-36 周的母亲参与者被随机分配接受 RSVpreF(120 或 240 μg ± 氢氧化铝)或安慰剂。结果:该分析包括来自 4 个国家(阿根廷、智利、南非、美国)的 579 名孕妇和 572 名婴儿;462 名母亲参与者接受了 RSVpreF。疫苗接种(母亲)或分娩(婴儿)后一个月的不良事件分别主要是妊娠期和新生儿期的预期事件。对于所有 RSVpreF 组,组合 RSV-A/-B 50% 中和滴度在 疫苗接种 后 2 周达到峰值。分娩时,RSVpreF 和安慰剂接受者的婴儿之间的几何平均滴度比为 10.9-13.6。经胎盘转移比率(所有组)为1.39-1.83。与安慰剂相比,母亲在生命的前 6 个月内接受 RSVpreF 的婴儿的中和几何平均滴度仍然较高,估计半衰期为 39-44 天。在一项探索性分析中,观察到联合 RSVpreF 组在出生后 180 天内对就医和就诊的严重婴儿 RSV 下呼吸道疾病的疗效(95% 置信区间)分别为 75%(-11%、94%)和 83%(-48%、99%)。结论:RSVpreF 具有良好的安全性,并通过有效的经胎盘转移引起强烈的中和反应。随后在关键的 3 期疗效试验中证实了预防婴儿 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估怀孕期间施用的二价 RSV prefusion F (RSVpreF) 疫苗 的安全性、免疫原性和潜在功效,解决了母亲 疫苗接种 预防婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病的需求,婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病是婴儿下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。
设计与对象
这是一项在 4 个国家(阿根廷、智利、南非、美国)进行的随机、安慰剂对照 2b 期试验,涉及 579 名孕妇和 572 名婴儿,其中孕产妇参与者在妊娠 24-36 周之间随机接受 RSVpreF(120 或 240 μg ± 氢氧化铝)或安慰剂。
核心方法
该试验通过母亲和婴儿的不良事件监测来评估安全性,使用 50% 中和滴度测量中和抗体反应,并评估分娩时的经胎盘抗体转移,并跟踪婴儿抗体在生命前 6 个月的持续情况。
主要结果
与安慰剂相比,RSVpreF 接受者在分娩时婴儿的几何平均滴度比为 10.9-13.6,经胎盘转移比为 1.39-1.83;婴儿中和抗体滴度在 6 个月内保持较高(估计半衰期为 39-44 天),并且在 180 天内对医疗护理和严重婴儿 RSV 下呼吸道疾病的探索性疗效分别为 75%(-11%、94%)和 83%(-48%、99%)。
解释与创新
作者将 RSVpreF 解释为具有良好的安全性,具有强大的免疫原性和有效的经胎盘抗体转移,支持其预防婴儿 RSV 疾病的潜力,这一发现后来在 3 期试验中得到证实。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持母体 RSV 疫苗接种 作为减少婴儿 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病的策略,为 疫苗 婴儿早期 RSV 预防政策和临床实践提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据受到 2b 期设计、小样本量、疗效估计置信区间宽以及对探索性分析的依赖的限制,需要在更大规模的 3 期试验中进行谨慎的解释和确认。

Phase 2 Randomized Bivalent RSVpreF Maternal Vaccine Trial: Final Safety, Antibody Persistence and Efficacy.

Authors: Eric A. F. Simões, Shabir A Madhi, Kimberly J Center, Alan T N Tita, Conrado Llapur, José M Novoa P, Kena A Swanson, David Radley, Emily A. Gomme, Linda G Brock
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: This report summarizes the final results from a randomized, placebo-controlled, phase 2b trial evaluating safety, antibody persistence, and potential efficacy of a bivalent respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prefusion F (RSVpreF) vaccine in pregnant individuals and their infants. METHODS: Maternal participants were randomized from 24-36 weeks gestation to receive RSVpreF (120 or 240 μg ± aluminum hydroxide) or placebo. RESULTS: This analysis included 579 pregnant individuals and 572 infants from 4 countries (Argentina, Chile, South Africa, United States); 462 maternal participants received RSVpreF. Adverse events in the month following vaccination (maternal) or birth (infant) were mostly anticipated events in pregnancy and the neonatal period, respectively. For all RSVpreF groups, combined RSV-A/-B 50% neutralizing titers peaked 2 weeks after vaccination. At delivery, geometric mean titer ratios between RSVpreF and placebo recipients' infants were 10.9-13.6. Transplacental transfer ratios (all groups) were 1.39-1.83. Neutralizing geometric mean titers remained higher in infants whose mothers received RSVpreF versus placebo through their first 6 months of life, with an estimated half-life of 39-44 days. In an exploratory analysis, observed efficacy (95% confidence interval) for the combined RSVpreF groups against medically attended and severe medically attended infant RSV lower respiratory tract illness through the first 180 days of life was 75% (-11%, 94%) and 83% (-48%, 99%), respectively. CONCLUSIONS: RSVpreF had a favorable safety profile and elicited robust neutralizing responses with efficient transplacental transfer. The potential to prevent infant RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness was subsequently confirmed in the pivotal phase 3 efficacy trial.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for maternal vaccination to prevent infant respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease, a leading cause of lower respiratory tract illness in infants, by evaluating the safety, immunogenicity, and potential efficacy of a bivalent RSV prefusion F (RSVpreF) vaccine administered during pregnancy.
Design and population
This was a randomized, placebo-controlled, phase 2b trial conducted across 4 countries (Argentina, Chile, South Africa, United States) involving 579 pregnant individuals and 572 infants, with maternal participants randomized between 24-36 weeks gestation to receive RSVpreF (120 or 240 μg ± aluminum hydroxide) or placebo.
Core methods
The trial assessed safety through adverse event monitoring in mothers and infants, measured neutralizing antibody responses using 50% neutralizing titers, and evaluated transplacental antibody transfer at delivery, with follow-up of infant antibody persistence through the first 6 months of life.
Main results
RSVpreF recipients showed geometric mean titer ratios of 10.9-13.6 in infants at delivery compared to placebo, with transplacental transfer ratios of 1.39-1.83; neutralizing antibody titers remained higher in infants through 6 months (estimated half-life 39-44 days), and exploratory efficacy against medically attended and severe infant RSV lower respiratory tract illness through 180 days was 75% (-11%, 94%) and 83% (-48%, 99%), respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted RSVpreF as having a favorable safety profile with robust immunogenicity and efficient transplacental antibody transfer, supporting its potential to prevent infant RSV disease, a finding later confirmed in a phase 3 trial.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support maternal RSV vaccination as a strategy to reduce infant RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness, informing vaccine policy and clinical practice for RSV prevention in early infancy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the phase 2b design, small sample size, wide confidence intervals for efficacy estimates, and reliance on exploratory analyses, warranting cautious interpretation and confirmation in larger phase 3 trials.

在患有或不患有血液肿瘤疾病的免疫功能低下患者中,疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 对抗呼吸道病毒的研究(响应):前瞻性 队列研究 研究方案。

Investigation of Vaccine-Induced Immune Response After Vaccination Against Respiratory Viruses in Immunocompromised Patients With or Without Hemato-Oncological Diseases (RESPONSE): Protocol for a Prospective Cohort Study.
作者： Eloísa Felipe Fumero, Jannik Stemler, Rosanne Sprute, Katja M Sauer, Henning Gruell, Christoph Kreer, Florian Klein, Sebastian J Theobald, Jan Rybniker, Oliver A. Cornely
摘要中文翻译背景:SARS-CoV-2 和流感等呼吸道病毒感染是重大的国际公共卫生问题。虽然癌症患者仍然是最脆弱的群体,但他们总体上对 疫苗 的反应较差。该人群的免疫学数据有限,主要集中在血清学参数上。然而,在这些患者中,细胞反应,尤其是 T 细胞反应似乎比体液反应更可靠地被诱导。目的:为了进一步了解 疫苗 诱导的免疫,RESPONSE 研究将分析早期和晚期加强剂 疫苗接种 对体液和细胞反应的影响,特别关注 T 细胞诱导的 免疫应答。此外,我们的目的是研究影响血液和肿瘤恶性肿瘤患者体液和细胞 疫苗 诱导免疫的因素,包括疾病状态、治疗 和人口因素。方法:通过使用标准化测定法测量结合和中和抗体来评估体液 免疫应答。将使用流式细胞术和 FluoroSpot 等功能测定来评估细胞免疫,并酌情使用其他探索性测定进行深入分析。免疫应答 将与临床参数相关,包括疾病状态、治疗 和人口统计因素。结果:这项研究在伦理批准后启动,目前正在招募参与者。招募于 2025 年 3 月 25 日开始,目前正在进行中,而大多数参与者的生物样本收集和随访已接近完成。自适应 免疫应答 的最终数据清理、数据集集成和统计分析计划从 2026 年第三季度开始。结论:本研究旨在为 疫苗接种 的结构化转化研究平台奠定基础,以实现最佳保护,免受不同呼吸道病原体的感染。长期目标是针对 疫苗 可预防疾病提供最佳保护,首先关注流感感染。此外,我们计划使用该平台研究 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 到最近批准的 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗,并可能在未来将其扩展到更多 疫苗。紧迫的问题,例如不同靶向疗法对 疫苗 免疫应答 的影响,将成为这些项目的一部分。试验注册:ClinicalTrials.gov NCT06612515;https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06612515。国际注册报告标识符 (IRRID):DERR1-10.2196/88520。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
SARS-CoV-2 和流感等呼吸道病毒感染是重大的国际公共卫生问题。虽然癌症患者仍然是最脆弱的群体,但他们总体上对 疫苗 的反应较差。
设计与对象
RESPONSE 研究是一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,重点关注患有或不患有血液肿瘤疾病的免疫功能低下患者。其目的是分析早期和晚期加强剂 疫苗接种 对体液和细胞反应的影响,特别关注 T 细胞诱导的 免疫应答。
核心方法
将通过使用标准化测定测量结合和中和抗体来评估体液 免疫应答。将使用流式细胞术和 FluoroSpot 等功能测定来评估细胞免疫,并酌情使用其他探索性测定进行深入分析。
主要结果
这项研究是在伦理委员会批准后启动的,目前正在招募参与者。招募于 2025 年 3 月 25 日开始,目前正在进行中,而大多数参与者的生物样本收集和随访已接近完成。
解释与创新
作者旨在研究影响血液学和肿瘤恶性肿瘤患者体液和细胞 疫苗 诱导的免疫的因素,包括疾病状态、治疗 和人口因素。
科研与公卫意义
该研究旨在为 疫苗接种 的结构化转化研究平台奠定基础,以实现最佳保护,免受不同呼吸道病原体的感染。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Investigation of Vaccine-Induced Immune Response After Vaccination Against Respiratory Viruses in Immunocompromised Patients With or Without Hemato-Oncological Diseases (RESPONSE): Protocol for a Prospective Cohort Study.

Authors: Eloísa Felipe Fumero, Jannik Stemler, Rosanne Sprute, Katja M Sauer, Henning Gruell, Christoph Kreer, Florian Klein, Sebastian J Theobald, Jan Rybniker, Oliver A. Cornely
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Infections with respiratory viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 and influenza are significant international public health concerns. While patients with cancer remain the most vulnerable group, they show poor vaccine response in general. Immunological data in this population are limited and mainly focus on serological parameters. However, in these patients, cellular, and especially T-cell, responses often seem to be induced more reliably than humoral responses. OBJECTIVE: To gain further insights into vaccine-induced immunity, the RESPONSE study will analyze the effect of early and late booster vaccination on humoral and cellular responses, with special focus on T cell-induced immune responses. In addition, we aim to investigate factors influencing humoral and cellular vaccine-induced immunity in patients with hematological and oncological malignancies, including state of disease, treatment, and demographic factors. METHODS: Humoral immune responses will be assessed by measuring binding and neutralizing antibodies using standardized assays. Cellular immunity will be evaluated using functional assays such as flow cytometry and FluoroSpot, as well as in-depth analyses using additional exploratory assays as appropriate. Immune responses will be correlated with clinical parameters, including disease status, treatment, and demographic factors. RESULTS: This study was initiated following ethics approval and is currently recruiting participants. Enrollment commenced on March 25, 2025, and is ongoing, whereas biosample collection and follow-up visits are nearing completion for most participants. Final data cleaning, dataset integration, and statistical analyses of adaptive immune responses are planned from the third quarter of 2026 onward. CONCLUSIONS: This study intends to lay a foundation for a structured translational research platform on vaccination to aim for best protection from infection by different respiratory pathogens. Long-term objectives are reaching best possible protection from vaccine-preventable disease with a first focus on influenza infection. In addition, we plan to investigate vaccine-induced immune responses to the recently approved respiratory syncytial virus vaccine using this platform and possibly extend this to further vaccines in the future. Urgent questions, such as the influence of different targeted therapies on vaccine immune response, will be part of these projects. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov NCT06612515; https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06612515. INTERNATIONAL REGISTERED REPORT IDENTIFIER (IRRID): DERR1-10.2196/88520.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infections with respiratory viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 and influenza are significant international public health concerns. While patients with cancer remain the most vulnerable group, they show poor vaccine response in general.
Design and population
The RESPONSE study is a prospective cohort study focusing on immunocompromised patients with or without hemato-oncological diseases. It aims to analyze the effect of early and late booster vaccination on humoral and cellular responses, with special focus on T cell-induced immune responses.
Core methods
Humoral immune responses will be assessed by measuring binding and neutralizing antibodies using standardized assays. Cellular immunity will be evaluated using functional assays such as flow cytometry and FluoroSpot, as well as in-depth analyses using additional exploratory assays as appropriate.
Main results
This study was initiated following ethics approval and is currently recruiting participants. Enrollment commenced on March 25, 2025, and is ongoing, whereas biosample collection and follow-up visits are nearing completion for most participants.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors aim to investigate factors influencing humoral and cellular vaccine-induced immunity in patients with hematological and oncological malignancies, including state of disease, treatment, and demographic factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study intends to lay a foundation for a structured translational research platform on vaccination to aim for best protection from infection by different respiratory pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

废水中 RSV 浓度以及儿童和老年人住院事件的趋势

RSV concentration in wastewater and trends among incident hospitalizations in children and older adults
作者： Dustin T. Hill, Jennifer Laplante, Sylvia Byun, Mohammed Alazawi, Donna L. Gowie, Sonali Biswas, Latavia Hill, Yifan Zhu, Monica R. Foote, Haley Kappus-Kron
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 有潜力填补临床 监测 中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 负担的空白。
设计与对象
使用从纽约四个县的 24 家 治疗 工厂收集的 2,662 个流入废水样本,测量了 RSV 浓度,并与 2022 年 9 月至 2024 年 7 月期间 RSV 住院情况进行比较。
核心方法
测量了废水中的 RSV 浓度,并将其与 RSV 住院情况进行比较,并使用预测模型来评估废水水平所解释的差异。
主要结果
废水中的 RSV 浓度与 RSV 住院率密切相关(ρ 在 0.52 至 0.85 之间,P <0.001),10 岁以下患者的住院率平均滞后三周,但对于 50 岁以上的患者来说,住院率最多滞后两周。
解释与创新
基于废水的 RSV 流行病学 是一种可靠的 监测 方法,可以为老年人群中 RSV 住院人数的增加提供早期预警。
科研与公卫意义
废水 监测 可以加强 RSV 监测,特别是对于老年人的早期检测,改善公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于高度相关性和预测能力,尽管其局限性包括超出研究区域和人群的潜在普遍性。

RSV concentration in wastewater and trends among incident hospitalizations in children and older adults

Authors: Dustin T. Hill, Jennifer Laplante, Sylvia Byun, Mohammed Alazawi, Donna L. Gowie, Sonali Biswas, Latavia Hill, Yifan Zhu, Monica R. Foote, Haley Kappus-Kron
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) has the potential to fill gaps in clinical surveillance for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) burden. Previous studies have shown RSV to be detectable in wastewater, but few have linked detections to clinical data because, in many locations, RSV is not a reportable disease. Further, studies that have linked RSV in wastewater to clinical data have not distinguished between pediatric and adult infections, as is common in studies of RSV. Using 2,662 influent wastewater samples collected from twenty-four treatment plants in four New York counties, we measured the RSV concentration in wastewater and compared levels detected to RSV hospitalizations from September 2022 to July 2024. RSV concentrations in wastewater correlated well with RSV hospitalizations (ρ between 0.52 and 0.85, P <0.001). RSV concentrations in wastewater lagged hospitalizations for under 10-year-olds by an average of three weeks but were a leading indicator for hospitalizations in patients over 50 by up to two weeks. In predictive models, wastewater explained 88 percent of the variance in RSV hospital admissions among people over 50. Wastewater-based epidemiology of RSV is therefore a reliable surveillance method and could provide early warning for increases in RSV hospitalizations among older populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) has the potential to fill gaps in clinical surveillance for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) burden.
Design and population
Using 2,662 influent wastewater samples collected from twenty-four treatment plants in four New York counties, RSV concentrations were measured and compared to RSV hospitalizations from September 2022 to July 2024.
Core methods
RSV concentrations in wastewater were measured and compared to RSV hospitalizations, with predictive models used to assess variance explained by wastewater levels.
Main results
RSV concentrations in wastewater correlated well with RSV hospitalizations (ρ between 0.52 and 0.85, P <0.001), lagging hospitalizations for under 10-year-olds by an average of three weeks but serving as a leading indicator for patients over 50 by up to two weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
Wastewater-based epidemiology of RSV is a reliable surveillance method that could provide early warning for increases in RSV hospitalizations among older populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Wastewater surveillance can enhance RSV monitoring, particularly for early detection in older adults, improving public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength is supported by high correlation and predictive power, though limitations include potential generalizability beyond the studied regions and populations.

单细胞病毒捕获识别 RSV 感染的气道类器官中的损伤重塑程序和 宿主 反应候选者

Single-cell viral capture identifies injury-remodeling programs and host-response candidates in RSV-infected airway organoids
作者： Rongrong Liu, Ziyu Liu, Pengbo Wang, Yao Zhang, Shiyuan Hou, Chenyu Zhang, Weina Guan, Shuhan Wang, Xing Sun, Chenxi Zhao
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致严重的儿科下呼吸道疾病,但将直接感染的气道细胞与暴露的旁观者细胞分开的 宿主 程序仍未完全解决。我们重新分析了来自感染 RSV 的人诱导多能干细胞衍生的呼吸类器官的公共单细胞 RNA 测序数据,并使用病毒转录本捕获来区分模拟细胞、RSV RNA 阴性旁观者细胞和 RSV RNA 阳性细胞。RSV 阳性细胞占图谱的 12.61%,显示出与病毒载量相关的应激、上皮损伤修复、旁分泌重塑、CD73/腺苷和代谢程序,而不仅仅是更强的干扰素反应。相比之下,旁观者细胞在干扰素刺激和 抗病毒药物 感知状态下富集。精心策划的配体受体分析、探索性虚拟扰动、多层 宿主 靶标排序和药物特征反转突出显示 IL11、PSAT1、GREM1、PTGS2、SERPINE1、NT5E/CD73 和 TNFRSF12A/Fn14 作为候选 宿主 节点。外部类器官批量 RNA-seq (GSE263272)、用作 治疗 终点参考的抗体处理类器官 scRNA-seq (GSE263271)、初级气道上皮数据 (GSE32139) 和内部 RSV 时程 qPCR 筛选支持损伤重塑模型,同时显示个体候选者因环境而异。计算机扰动将 NT5E/CD73 置于网络相关的二级节点中,并将 SYK 抑制剂 Fostamatinib/R406 在药物特征逆转中排名靠前。这些发现为后续实验设定了优先顺序,但并未确定功能性救援或治疗效果。总之,该研究定义了一组用于功能筛选的重点 RSV 类器官 宿主 反应候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致严重的儿科下呼吸道疾病,但将直接感染的气道细胞与暴露的旁观者细胞分开的 宿主 程序仍未完全解决。
设计与对象
我们重新分析了来自感染 RSV 的人诱导多能干细胞衍生的呼吸类器官的公共单细胞 RNA 测序数据,并使用病毒转录本捕获来区分模拟细胞、RSV RNA 阴性旁观者细胞和 RSV RNA 阳性细胞。
核心方法
精心策划的配体受体分析、探索性虚拟扰动、多层 宿主 靶标排序和药物特征反转突出显示 IL11、PSAT1、GREM1、PTGS2、SERPINE1、NT5E/CD73 和 TNFRSF12A/Fn14 作为候选 宿主 节点。
主要结果
RSV 阳性细胞占图谱的 12.61%,显示出与病毒载量相关的应激、上皮损伤修复、旁分泌重塑、CD73/腺苷和代谢程序,而不仅仅是更强的干扰素反应。相比之下,旁观者细胞在干扰素刺激和 抗病毒药物 感知状态下富集。
解释与创新
这些发现为后续实验设定了优先顺序,但并未确定功能性救援或治疗效果。总之,该研究定义了一组用于功能筛选的重点 RSV 类器官 宿主 反应候选者。
科研与公卫意义
外部类器官批量 RNA-seq (GSE263272)、用作 治疗 终点参考的抗体处理类器官 scRNA-seq (GSE263271)、初级气道上皮数据 (GSE32139) 和内部 RSV 时程 qPCR 筛选支持损伤重塑模型,同时显示个体候选者因环境而异。
局限与证据强度
计算机扰动将 NT5E/CD73 置于网络相关的二级节点中,并将 SYK 抑制剂 Fostamatinib/R406 在药物特征逆转中排名靠前。由于依赖计算预测且缺乏功能验证,证据强度中等。

Single-cell viral capture identifies injury-remodeling programs and host-response candidates in RSV-infected airway organoids

Authors: Rongrong Liu, Ziyu Liu, Pengbo Wang, Yao Zhang, Shiyuan Hou, Chenyu Zhang, Weina Guan, Shuhan Wang, Xing Sun, Chenxi Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes substantial pediatric lower respiratory tract disease, but the host programs that separate directly infected airway cells from exposed bystander cells are still not fully resolved. We reanalyzed public single-cell RNA sequencing data from RSV-infected human induced pluripotent stem cell-derived respiratory organoids and used viral transcript capture to distinguish mock cells, RSV RNA-negative bystander cells and RSV RNA-positive cells. RSV-positive cells made up 12.61% of the atlas and showed viral-load-linked stress, epithelial injury-repair, paracrine remodeling, CD73/adenosine and metabolic programs, not merely stronger interferon responses. Bystander cells, by contrast, were enriched for interferon-stimulated and antiviral sensing states. Curated ligand-receptor analysis, exploratory virtual perturbation, multi-layer host-target ranking and drug-signature reversal highlighted IL11, PSAT1, GREM1, PTGS2, SERPINE1, NT5E/CD73 and TNFRSF12A/Fn14 as candidate host nodes. External organoid bulk RNA-seq (GSE263272), antibody-treated organoid scRNA-seq used as a treatment-endpoint reference (GSE263271), primary airway epithelial data (GSE32139) and an in-house RSV time-course qPCR screen supported the injury-remodeling model, while showing that individual candidates vary by context. In silico perturbation placed NT5E/CD73 among network-relevant secondary-tier nodes and ranked the SYK inhibitor Fostamatinib/R406 highly in drug-signature reversal. These findings set priorities for experimental follow-up but do not establish functional rescue or therapeutic efficacy. Together, the study defines a focused set of RSV organoid host-response candidates for functional screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes substantial pediatric lower respiratory tract disease, but the host programs that separate directly infected airway cells from exposed bystander cells are still not fully resolved.
Design and population
We reanalyzed public single-cell RNA sequencing data from RSV-infected human induced pluripotent stem cell-derived respiratory organoids and used viral transcript capture to distinguish mock cells, RSV RNA-negative bystander cells and RSV RNA-positive cells.
Core methods
Curated ligand-receptor analysis, exploratory virtual perturbation, multi-layer host-target ranking and drug-signature reversal highlighted IL11, PSAT1, GREM1, PTGS2, SERPINE1, NT5E/CD73 and TNFRSF12A/Fn14 as candidate host nodes.
Main results
RSV-positive cells made up 12.61% of the atlas and showed viral-load-linked stress, epithelial injury-repair, paracrine remodeling, CD73/adenosine and metabolic programs, not merely stronger interferon responses. Bystander cells, by contrast, were enriched for interferon-stimulated and antiviral sensing states.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings set priorities for experimental follow-up but do not establish functional rescue or therapeutic efficacy. Together, the study defines a focused set of RSV organoid host-response candidates for functional screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
External organoid bulk RNA-seq (GSE263272), antibody-treated organoid scRNA-seq used as a treatment-endpoint reference (GSE263271), primary airway epithelial data (GSE32139) and an in-house RSV time-course qPCR screen supported the injury-remodeling model, while showing that individual candidates vary by context.
Limitations and evidence strength
In silico perturbation placed NT5E/CD73 among network-relevant secondary-tier nodes and ranked the SYK inhibitor Fostamatinib/R406 highly in drug-signature reversal. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on computational predictions and lack of functional validation.

第二季中的 尼塞韦单抗 和下呼吸道感染住院治疗。

Nirsevimab and Hospitalization for Lower Respiratory Tract Infection During the Second Season.
作者： David Moreno-Pérez, Esther Catalán-Fernández, Borja Croche-Santander, Jesús Miguel Ríos-Hurtado, Olga Díaz-Morales, Leticia Martínez-Campos, Elena Pérez-González, María Del Carmen Martínez-Padilla, Juan Luis Santos-Pérez, María Teresa Santos-Martín
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
评估 尼塞韦单抗 在出生后第一个月服用 尼塞韦单抗 的婴儿在生命第二个呼吸道季节预防下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 住院的效果。
设计与对象
在西班牙南部安达卢西亚的 9 家三级儿科医院进行了基于社区的病例对照研究,包括 2023 年 4 月 1 日至 2024 年 3 月 31 日期间出生的婴儿,病例为 2024 年至 2025 年季节期间因 LRTI 住院的婴儿。
核心方法
对于每个病例,4 个社区对照按出生日期和省份进行匹配;暴露于2023年至2024年呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)季节期间的尼塞韦单抗管理;主要结果是 2024 年至 2025 年季节期间因 LRTI 住院;使用逆概率加权 (IPW) 和双稳健增强 IPW (AIPW) 模型来估计平均 治疗 效应,表示为绝对风险降低 (ARR)。
主要结果
The study included 1375 infants (275 cases and 1100 controls); nirsevimab administration was associated with a 17.0% ARR (95% CI, 3.2%-30.8%) in LRTI hospitalizations during the 2024 to 2025 season in the AIPW model; stronger associations were observed among RSV-positive cases (ARR = 25.4%; 95% CI, 4.9%-45.8%) and in infants with a history of inhaler use (ARR = 27.7%; 95% CI, 6.4%-48.9%); a negative control exposure (rotavirus vaccination) showed no association, supporting no evidence of relevant residual confounding regarding health-seeking behaviors.
解释与创新
尼塞韦单抗 给药与第二季 LRTI 住院治疗的减少有关,这表明在给药季节之后具有有意义的益处,并对未来的成本效益评估具有潜在影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,尼塞韦单抗 在减少第二个呼吸季节 LRTI 住院治疗方面具有潜在的延伸效益,支持其在婴儿 RSV 预防计划和未来成本效益分析中的使用。
局限与证据强度
研究设计为病例对照研究,可能会引入选择偏倚;然而,使用逆概率加权和阴性对照暴露可以强化证据,从而对证据强度进行适度评估。

Nirsevimab and Hospitalization for Lower Respiratory Tract Infection During the Second Season.

Authors: David Moreno-Pérez, Esther Catalán-Fernández, Borja Croche-Santander, Jesús Miguel Ríos-Hurtado, Olga Díaz-Morales, Leticia Martínez-Campos, Elena Pérez-González, María Del Carmen Martínez-Padilla, Juan Luis Santos-Pérez, María Teresa Santos-Martín
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To assess the effect of nirsevimab in preventing hospitalizations for lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) during the second respiratory season of life among infants who were administered nirsevimab in their first months of life. METHODS: Community-based case-control study conducted in 9 tertiary pediatric hospitals in Andalusia, Southern Spain. Infants born between April 1, 2023, and March 31, 2024, were included. Cases were infants hospitalized for LRTI during the 2024 to 2025 season. For each case, 4 community controls were matched by birthdate and province. Exposure was nirsevimab administration during the 2023 to 2024 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) season. The main outcome was hospitalization for LRTI during the 2024 to 2025 season. Inverse probability weighting (IPW) and doubly robust augmented IPW (AIPW) models were used to estimate the average treatment effect, expressed as absolute risk reduction (ARR). RESULTS: The study included 1375 infants (275 cases and 1100 controls). Nirsevimab administration during the 2023 to 2024 season was associated with a 17.0% ARR (95% CI, 3.2%-30.8%) in LRTI hospitalizations during the 2024 to 2025 season in the AIPW model. Stronger associations were observed among RSV-positive cases (ARR = 25.4%; 95% CI, 4.9%-45.8%) and in infants with a history of inhaler use (ARR = 27.7%; 95% CI, 6.4%-48.9%). A negative control exposure (rotavirus vaccination) showed no association, supporting no evidence of relevant residual confounding regarding health-seeking behaviors. CONCLUSIONS: Nirsevimab administration was associated with a reduction in second-season LRTI hospitalizations, suggesting meaningful benefits beyond the season of administration and potential implications for future cost-effectiveness evaluations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To assess the effect of nirsevimab in preventing hospitalizations for lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) during the second respiratory season of life among infants who were administered nirsevimab in their first months of life.
Design and population
Community-based case-control study conducted in 9 tertiary pediatric hospitals in Andalusia, Southern Spain, including infants born between April 1, 2023, and March 31, 2024, with cases being infants hospitalized for LRTI during the 2024 to 2025 season.
Core methods
For each case, 4 community controls were matched by birthdate and province; exposure was nirsevimab administration during the 2023 to 2024 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) season; the main outcome was hospitalization for LRTI during the 2024 to 2025 season; inverse probability weighting (IPW) and doubly robust augmented IPW (AIPW) models were used to estimate the average treatment effect, expressed as absolute risk reduction (ARR).
Main results
The study included 1375 infants (275 cases and 1100 controls); nirsevimab administration was associated with a 17.0% ARR (95% CI, 3.2%-30.8%) in LRTI hospitalizations during the 2024 to 2025 season in the AIPW model; stronger associations were observed among RSV-positive cases (ARR = 25.4%; 95% CI, 4.9%-45.8%) and in infants with a history of inhaler use (ARR = 27.7%; 95% CI, 6.4%-48.9%); a negative control exposure (rotavirus vaccination) showed no association, supporting no evidence of relevant residual confounding regarding health-seeking behaviors.
Interpretation and novelty
Nirsevimab administration was associated with a reduction in second-season LRTI hospitalizations, suggesting meaningful benefits beyond the season of administration and potential implications for future cost-effectiveness evaluations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the potential extended benefits of nirsevimab in reducing LRTI hospitalizations during the second respiratory season, supporting its use in infant RSV prevention programs and future cost-effectiveness analyses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design is a case-control study, which may introduce selection bias; however, the use of inverse probability weighting and a negative control exposure strengthens the evidence, resulting in a moderate assessment of evidence strength.

意大利米兰的呼吸道病毒综合医院、急诊科和社区 监测。

Integrated Hospital, Emergency Department, and Community Surveillance for Respiratory Viruses in Milan, Italy.
作者： Alberto Rizzo, Federica Salari, Cristina Galli, Manuel Maffeo, Cristina Paduraru, Simone Villa, Alberto Dolci, Elena Pariani, Danilo Cereda, Milan Respiratory Viruses Surveillance Working Group
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒的传播可能因医疗机构和年龄组的不同而有所不同,这可能会限制基于单一来源的 监测 的解释性。在意大利米兰,已建立的基于社区和急诊科的系统与医院实验室检测并存,但医院病房级数据并未定期整合。本研究的目的是描述呼吸道病毒的 流行病学 并评估基于病房的 监测 是否与社区和 ED 监测 相补充。我们分析了 2024 年 1 月 1 日(W1/2024)至 2025 年 10 月 12 日(W41/2025)在社区、急诊室和医院病房收集的呼吸道样本的分子检测结果。病毒包括甲型流感 (IAV)、乙型流感 (IBV)、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2(其中包含扩展组)。在 8029 个样本中,≥ 1 种病毒的总体阳性率为 46.8%,急诊科 (51.1%) 和社区 (50.5%) 最高,病房较低 (36.7%) (p < 0.001)。2024-2025年期间,IAV首先在病房(W33/2024)中检测到,并且在病房(W2/2025)中比社区(W3/2025)和急诊室(W4/2025)更早达到峰值。IBV 首次在急诊科 (W36/2024) 中检测到,并在急诊科 (W5/2025)、社区 (W7/2025) 和病房 (W8/2025) 中达到高峰。RSV 首先在社区 (W37/2024) 中重新出现,并在社区/急诊科 (W50/2024) 中达到顶峰,随后在病房 (W2/2025) 中达到顶峰。不同医疗机构和年龄组的呼吸道病毒阳性率和描述性高峰时间有所不同。以病房为基础的 监测 为住院和老年人群提供了补充信息,但它并不总是比社区或 ED 监测 早或优于。当与测试人群、测试适应症和临床结果可用性的差异一起解释时,集成的多设置 监测 可以提高态势感知。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于病房的 监测 是否可以补充社区和急诊科 (ED) 监测 的呼吸道病毒。呼吸道病毒的传播可能因医疗机构和年龄组的不同而有所不同,这可能会限制基于单一来源的 监测 的解释性。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2024 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 10 月 12 日在意大利米兰社区、急诊室和医院病房收集的呼吸道样本的分子检测结果。病毒包括甲型流感 (IAV)、乙型流感 (IBV)、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2(其中包含扩展组)。
核心方法
该研究整合了在多个医疗机构(包括社区、急诊室和医院病房)进行的分子测试的数据,以评估呼吸道病毒阳性率和高峰时间。
主要结果
在 8029 个样本中,≥ 1 种病毒的总体阳性率为 46.8%,急诊科 (51.1%) 和社区 (50.5%) 最高,病房较低 (36.7%) (p < 0.001)。RSV 首先在社区 (W37/2024) 中重新出现,并在社区/急诊科 (W50/2024) 中达到顶峰,随后在病房 (W2/2025) 中达到顶峰。
解释与创新
作者解释说,不同医疗机构和年龄组的呼吸道病毒阳性率和描述性高峰时间有所不同,这凸显了综合、多环境 监测 的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,综合的、多设置的 监测 可以提高对呼吸道病毒的态势感知,特别是在考虑测试人群、测试适应症和临床结果可用性的差异时。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其对分子测试结果的依赖以及不同环境下测试实践的差异可能会影响可解释性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Integrated Hospital, Emergency Department, and Community Surveillance for Respiratory Viruses in Milan, Italy.

Authors: Alberto Rizzo, Federica Salari, Cristina Galli, Manuel Maffeo, Cristina Paduraru, Simone Villa, Alberto Dolci, Elena Pariani, Danilo Cereda, Milan Respiratory Viruses Surveillance Working Group
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory virus circulation may vary across healthcare settings and age groups, potentially limiting the interpretability of surveillance based on a single source. In Milan, Italy, established community- and emergency department-based systems coexist with hospital laboratory testing, but hospital ward-level data are not routinely integrated. The aims of this study were to describe respiratory viruses' epidemiology and assess whether ward-based surveillance complements community and ED surveillance. We analyzed molecular test results from respiratory samples collected from January 1, 2024 (W1/2024) to October 12, 2025 (W41/2025) in community, ED, and hospital ward settings. Viruses included influenza A (IAV), influenza B (IBV), RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 (with extended panels in a subset). Among 8029 samples, overall positivity for ≥ 1 virus was 46.8%, highest in ED (51.1%) and community (50.5%) and lower in wards (36.7%) (p < 0.001). During 2024-2025, IAV was first detected in wards (W33/2024) and peaked earlier in wards (W2/2025) than community (W3/2025) and ED (W4/2025). IBV was first detected in ED (W36/2024) and peaked in ED (W5/2025), community (W7/2025), and wards (W8/2025). RSV reappeared first in community (W37/2024) and peaked in community/ED (W50/2024) and later in wards (W2/2025). Respiratory virus positivity and descriptive peak timing differed across healthcare settings and age groups. Ward-based surveillance provided complementary information for hospitalized and older populations, but it was not consistently earlier or superior to community or ED surveillance. Integrated, multi-setting surveillance can improve situational awareness when interpreted alongside differences in tested populations, testing indications, and clinical outcome availability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether ward-based surveillance complements community and emergency department (ED) surveillance for respiratory viruses. Respiratory virus circulation may vary across healthcare settings and age groups, potentially limiting the interpretability of surveillance based on a single source.
Design and population
The study analyzed molecular test results from respiratory samples collected in community, ED, and hospital ward settings in Milan, Italy, from January 1, 2024, to October 12, 2025. Viruses included influenza A (IAV), influenza B (IBV), RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 (with extended panels in a subset).
Core methods
The study integrated data from molecular tests conducted across multiple healthcare settings, including community, ED, and hospital wards, to assess respiratory virus positivity and peak timing.
Main results
Among 8029 samples, overall positivity for ≥ 1 virus was 46.8%, highest in ED (51.1%) and community (50.5%) and lower in wards (36.7%) (p < 0.001). RSV reappeared first in community (W37/2024) and peaked in community/ED (W50/2024) and later in wards (W2/2025).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that respiratory virus positivity and descriptive peak timing differed across healthcare settings and age groups, highlighting the value of integrated, multi-setting surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrated, multi-setting surveillance can improve situational awareness for respiratory viruses, particularly when considering differences in tested populations, testing indications, and clinical outcome availability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on molecular test results and the potential for differences in testing practices across settings to affect interpretability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

潮式呼吸分析为客观评估急性呼吸力学和支气管扩张剂反应性提供了一种非侵入性床旁方法 毛细支气管炎

Tidal Breathing Analysis Provides a Non-Invasive, Bedside Method for Objectively Assessing Respiratory Mechanics and Bronchodilator Responsiveness in Acute Bronchiolitis
作者： Mihai Craiu, Radu Gheorghiu, Alexandra Bratu, Iustina Stan
摘要中文翻译背景:急性 毛细支气管炎 是婴儿下呼吸道感染的最常见原因之一,并且仍然是一种临床异质综合征。目前的国际指南建议支持性治疗,通常不鼓励常规使用支气管扩张剂;然而,部分患者可能存在生理反应。潮式呼吸分析 (TBA) 提供了一种非侵入性床旁方法,用于客观评估呼吸力学,并可以客观量化支气管扩张剂给药后的生理变化。病例介绍 我们报告一名 23 个月大的男孩,患有严重的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 样喘息疾病,并发急性呼吸衰竭。在吸入支气管扩张剂给药之前和之后使用预定义的 TBA 参数评估支气管扩张剂反应性。治疗 后,潮气呼气流量峰值时间 (tPTEF) 从 0.17 秒增加至 0.33 秒 (+94%),而 tPTEF/TE 比率从 0.12 增加至 0.27 (+125%)。呼气时间 (TE) 和总呼吸周期持续时间 (Ttot) 分别减少 12.6% 和 5.1%,而潮气量 (VT) 和每分钟通气量 (MV) 增加 12.1% 和 18.3%。呼气峰流量保持不变(支气管扩张剂给药前后均为 0.14 L/s)。讨论 据我们所知,这是使用床边 TBA 来表征严重 毛细支气管炎 样疾病的急性支气管扩张剂反应性的首批报告之一。呼气正时指数的改善,伴随着呼气时间的缩短而呼气峰流量没有变化,与支气管扩张剂给药后呼气流量动力学的改善一致。这些发现支持了可能存在支气管扩张剂反应性生理表型的假设。先前的研究已证明婴儿期严重的 毛细支气管炎 与随后的复发性喘息或哮喘之间存在关联;然而,通过 TBA 客观测量的急性支气管扩张反应是否代表了未来纵向研究的可检验假设。结论 该病例说明了床边 TBA 确定严重 毛细支气管炎 样疾病中生理性支气管扩张剂反应性的可行性。有必要进行前瞻性纵向研究,以确定 TBA 定义的反应性是否代表需要前瞻性验证的早期生理标志,以及这种生理分层是否可以支持个体化的随访策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性 毛细支气管炎 是婴儿期常见且临床异质性的下呼吸道感染,虽然目前的指南不鼓励常规使用支气管扩张剂,但某些患者可能存在生理反应。潮式呼吸分析 (TBA) 提供了一种非侵入性床边方法,可客观评估呼吸力学并量化支气管扩张剂给药后的生理变化。
设计与对象
本案例研究报告了一名患有严重 RSV 毛细支气管炎 样喘息疾病并并发急性呼吸衰竭的 23 个月大男孩,在吸入支气管扩张剂给药前后使用预定义的 TBA 参数评估了该男孩的支气管扩张剂反应性。
核心方法
使用潮气呼吸分析(TBA)评估支气管扩张剂反应性,以测量预定义参数,包括潮气呼气流量峰值时间(tPTEF)、tPTEF/TE比值、呼气时间(TE)、总呼吸周期持续时间(Ttot)、潮气量(VT)、每分钟通气量(MV)以及支气管扩张剂给药前后的峰值呼气流量。
主要结果
治疗 后,tPTEF 从 0.17 增加到 0.33 s (+94%),tPTEF/TE 比率从 0.12 增加到 0.27 (+125%)。TE 和 Ttot 分别下降 12.6% 和 5.1%,而 VT 和 MV 分别增加 12.1% 和 18.3%。呼气峰值流量保持不变,为 0.14 L/s。
解释与创新
这是使用床边 TBA 来表征严重 毛细支气管炎 样疾病的急性支气管扩张剂反应性的首批报告之一,呼气计时指数的改善与呼气流量动力学的改善一致,支持支气管扩张剂反应性生理表型的假设。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,TBA 定义的支气管扩张剂反应性可能代表严重 毛细支气管炎 的早期生理标志,可能支持个体化 治疗 策略,并为未来关于其对复发性喘息或哮喘的预测价值的纵向研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
这是一个单一案例研究,限制了普遍性和统计能力,但客观的测量和明确的生理变化为 TBA 评估严重 毛细支气管炎 支气管扩张剂反应性的可行性提供了适度的证据。

Tidal Breathing Analysis Provides a Non-Invasive, Bedside Method for Objectively Assessing Respiratory Mechanics and Bronchodilator Responsiveness in Acute Bronchiolitis

Authors: Mihai Craiu, Radu Gheorghiu, Alexandra Bratu, Iustina Stan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Acute bronchiolitis is among the most common causes of lower respiratory tract infection in infancy and remains a clinically heterogeneous syndrome. Current international guidelines recommend supportive management and generally discourage routine bronchodilator administration; however, physiological responsiveness may exist in a subset of patients. Tidal breathing analysis (TBA) provides a non-invasive, bedside method for objectively assessing respiratory mechanics and may objectively quantify physiological changes following bronchodilator administration. Case Presentation We report a 23-month-old boy presenting with severe RSV bronchiolitis-like wheezing illness complicated by acute respiratory failure. Bronchodilator responsiveness was evaluated using predefined TBA parameters before and after inhaled bronchodilator administration. Following treatment, time to peak tidal expiratory flow (tPTEF) increased from 0.17 to 0.33 s (+94%), while the tPTEF/TE ratio increased from 0.12 to 0.27 (+125%). Expiratory time (TE) and total respiratory cycle duration (Ttot) decreased by 12.6% and 5.1%, respectively, whereas tidal volume (VT) and minute ventilation (MV) increased by 12.1% and 18.3%. Peak expiratory flow remained unchanged (0.14 L/s before and after bronchodilator administration). Discussion To our knowledge, this is among the first reports using bedside TBA to characterize acute bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis-like illness. The improvement in expiratory timing indices, accompanied by shorter expiratory time without changes in peak expiratory flow, is consistent with improved expiratory flow dynamics following bronchodilator administration. These findings support the hypothesis that a bronchodilator-responsive physiological phenotype may exist. Previous studies have demonstrated an association between severe bronchiolitis during infancy and subsequent recurrent wheezing or asthma; however, whether acute bronchodilator responsiveness, measured objectively by TBA, represents a testable hypothesis for future longitudinal studies. Conclusions This case illustrates the feasibility of bedside TBA for identifying physiological bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis-like illness. Prospective longitudinal studies are warranted to determine whether TBA-defined responsiveness represents an early physiological marker requiring prospective validation and whether such physiological stratification may support individualized follow-up strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute bronchiolitis is a common and clinically heterogeneous lower respiratory tract infection in infancy, and while current guidelines discourage routine bronchodilator use, physiological responsiveness may exist in some patients. Tidal breathing analysis (TBA) offers a non-invasive, bedside method to objectively assess respiratory mechanics and quantify physiological changes after bronchodilator administration.
Design and population
This case study reports on a 23-month-old boy with severe RSV bronchiolitis-like wheezing illness complicated by acute respiratory failure, in whom bronchodilator responsiveness was evaluated using predefined TBA parameters before and after inhaled bronchodilator administration.
Core methods
Bronchodilator responsiveness was assessed using tidal breathing analysis (TBA) to measure predefined parameters including time to peak tidal expiratory flow (tPTEF), tPTEF/TE ratio, expiratory time (TE), total respiratory cycle duration (Ttot), tidal volume (VT), minute ventilation (MV), and peak expiratory flow before and after bronchodilator administration.
Main results
Following treatment, tPTEF increased from 0.17 to 0.33 s (+94%), and the tPTEF/TE ratio increased from 0.12 to 0.27 (+125%). TE and Ttot decreased by 12.6% and 5.1%, respectively, while VT and MV increased by 12.1% and 18.3%. Peak expiratory flow remained unchanged at 0.14 L/s.
Interpretation and novelty
This is among the first reports using bedside TBA to characterize acute bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis-like illness, with improvements in expiratory timing indices consistent with improved expiratory flow dynamics, supporting the hypothesis of a bronchodilator-responsive physiological phenotype.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TBA-defined bronchodilator responsiveness may represent an early physiological marker in severe bronchiolitis, potentially supporting individualized treatment strategies and informing future longitudinal studies on its predictive value for recurrent wheezing or asthma.
Limitations and evidence strength
This is a single case study, limiting generalizability and statistical power, but the objective measurements and clear physiological changes provide moderate evidence for the feasibility of TBA in assessing bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis.
