2023-2025 年保加利亚流行的 患病率 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 的遗传多样性

Prevalence and genetic diversity of respiratory syncytial viruses circulating in Bulgaria, 2023-2025
作者： Neli Korsun, Ivelina Trifonova, Yordanka Uzunova, Petar Velikov, Diyana Pavlova, Iliana Grigorova, Ivan Ivanov, Daniel Ivanov, Silviya Voleva, Iva Christova
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性呼吸道感染 (ARIs) 的主要原因,对 5 岁以下儿童造成相当大的疾病负担。本研究旨在表征 RSV 的 患病率、循环毒株的遗传多样性以及病毒蛋白的氨基酸变异性,并鉴定批准用于 RSV 预防的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的结合位点内的 突变。方法 使用多重实时 PCR 对 5,170 名各个年龄段的 ARI 患者的鼻咽标本进行 13 种呼吸道病毒检测。对代表性 RSV 阳性样本进行全 基因组 测序,然后进行系统发育和氨基酸序列分析。结果 303例(5.9%)患者RSV呈阳性,其中61例(20.1%)合并感染其他呼吸道病毒。在 5 岁以下儿童中,RSV 是 毛细支气管炎(30.6%)和肺炎(12.6%)病例中最常检测到的病原体。对 2024-2025 年季节的 RSV 阳性样本进行了基因分型,观察到 RSV-A 相对于 RSV-B 的优势。系统发育分析分别鉴定出 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 内的 8 个和 7 个遗传谱系,其中 A.D.1.11 和 B.D.1.1 最为普遍。在 RSV-A 的 F 蛋白抗原位点(Ø、I-V)中未检测到替换,包括 尼塞韦单抗(位点 Ø)、帕利珠单抗(位点 II)和 clesrovimab(位点 IV)的结合位点。相反,RSV-B 株在抗原位点 Ø (n=3)、I (n=2) 和 V (n=2) 中表现出取代。结论 RSV 是幼儿期严重呼吸道疾病的主要原因,并且循环毒株之间存在显着的遗传多样性。F 蛋白中不存在与耐药性相关的取代,表明目前流行的 RSV 菌株仍然对批准的 mAb 敏感。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性呼吸道感染 (ARIs) 的主要原因,对 5 岁以下儿童造成相当大的疾病负担。
设计与对象
本研究旨在表征 RSV 的 患病率、循环毒株的遗传多样性以及病毒蛋白的氨基酸变异性,并鉴定批准用于 RSV 预防的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的结合位点内的 突变。
核心方法
使用多重实时 PCR 对 5,170 名各个年龄段的 ARI 患者的鼻咽标本进行了 13 种呼吸道病毒检测。对代表性 RSV 阳性样本进行全 基因组 测序,然后进行系统发育和氨基酸序列分析。
主要结果
303 例(5.9%)患者 RSV 呈阳性,其中 61 例(20.1%)合并感染其他呼吸道病毒。在 5 岁以下儿童中,RSV 是 毛细支气管炎(30.6%)和肺炎(12.6%)病例中最常检测到的病原体。
解释与创新
F 蛋白中不存在与耐药性相关的取代,表明目前流行的 RSV 菌株仍然对批准的 mAb 敏感。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解 RSV 毒株的遗传多样性及其对单克隆抗体的敏感性提供了重要见解,这可以为 监测、诊断 和针对性干预措施的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 2024-2025 年季节的数据,并未考虑长期趋势或季节变化。证据是强有力的,因为它基于 RSV 阳性样本的直接测试和测序。

Prevalence and genetic diversity of respiratory syncytial viruses circulating in Bulgaria, 2023-2025

Authors: Neli Korsun, Ivelina Trifonova, Yordanka Uzunova, Petar Velikov, Diyana Pavlova, Iliana Grigorova, Ivan Ivanov, Daniel Ivanov, Silviya Voleva, Iva Christova
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of acute respiratory infections (ARIs) with a considerable disease burden among children under 5 years of age. This study aimed to characterize the prevalence of RSV, genetic diversity of circulating strains, and amino acid variability in viral proteins, as well as identify mutations within the binding sites of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) approved for RSV prophylaxis. Methods Nasopharyngeal specimens from 5,170 patients of all ages with ARI were tested for 13 respiratory viruses using multiplex real-time PCR. Representative RSV-positive samples were subjected to whole-genome sequencing, followed by phylogenetic and amino acid sequence analyses. Results RSV was positive in 303 (5.9%) patients, of whom 61 (20.1%) had co-infections with other respiratory viruses. Among children under 5 years of age, RSV was the most frequently detected pathogen in cases of bronchiolitis (30.6%) and pneumonia (12.6%). RSV-positive samples from the 2024-2025 season were genotyped, and predominance of RSV-A over RSV-B was observed. Phylogenetic analysis identified eight and seven genetic lineages within RSV-A and RSV-B, respectively, with A.D.1.11 and B.D.1.1 being the most prevalent. No substitutions were detected in the F protein antigenic sites of RSV-A (Ø, I-V), including the binding sites for nirsevimab (site Ø), palivizumab (site II), and clesrovimab (site IV). Conversely, RSV-B strains exhibited substitutions in antigenic sites Ø (n=3), I (n=2), and V (n=2). Conclusions RSV is a leading cause of severe respiratory disease in early childhood and demonstrates substantial genetic diversity among circulating strains. The absence of resistance-associated substitutions in the F protein indicates that currently circulating RSV strains remain susceptible to approved mAbs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of acute respiratory infections (ARIs) with a considerable disease burden among children under 5 years of age.
Design and population
This study aimed to characterize the prevalence of RSV, genetic diversity of circulating strains, and amino acid variability in viral proteins, as well as identify mutations within the binding sites of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) approved for RSV prophylaxis.
Core methods
Nasopharyngeal specimens from 5,170 patients of all ages with ARI were tested for 13 respiratory viruses using multiplex real-time PCR. Representative RSV-positive samples were subjected to whole-genome sequencing, followed by phylogenetic and amino acid sequence analyses.
Main results
RSV was positive in 303 (5.9%) patients, of whom 61 (20.1%) had co-infections with other respiratory viruses. Among children under 5 years of age, RSV was the most frequently detected pathogen in cases of bronchiolitis (30.6%) and pneumonia (12.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
The absence of resistance-associated substitutions in the F protein indicates that currently circulating RSV strains remain susceptible to approved mAbs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide important insights into the genetic diversity of RSV strains and their susceptibility to monoclonal antibodies, which can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and the development of targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to data from the 2024-2025 season and does not account for long-term trends or seasonal variations. The evidence is strong as it is based on direct testing and sequencing of RSV-positive samples.

意大利 进化 实施第一季(2024-2025 年)期间 RSV 的基因组 进化 和少数变异动态

Genomic Evolution and Minority Variant Dynamics of RSV during the First Season of Nirsevimab Implementation in Italy (2024-2025)
作者： Greta Romano, Roberta Campagna, Guglielmo Ferrari, Antonino Maria Guglielmo Pitrolo, Ombretta Turriziani, Matteo Fracella, Eleonora Coratti, Lilia Cinti, Piergiorgio Roberto, Petrarca Laura
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述 2024-2025 年 RSV 季节期间在意大利两个地区流行的 RSV 菌株的整个 基因组 的特征,这是实施国家 尼塞韦单抗 预防计划后的第一个,因为对 宿主 之间和内部水平的遗传变异的调查有限。
设计与对象
对来自未接受 基因组 预防的患者的 88 份呼吸道样本进行了完整的 基因组 测序,这些样本的 RSV 检测呈阳性。
核心方法
开发了一种新设计的生物信息学管道,用于评估 宿主 内的遗传变异,并揭示 RSV 谱系中少数 突变 的分布,并通过系统发育推断将菌株与欧洲循环相关联。
主要结果
RSV-A (n=54) 显示出显着高于 RSV-B (n=34) 的核苷酸多样性,其中 A.D.3 是最常见的 RSV-A 谱系 (64%),而所有 RSV-B 株均为 B.D.E.;G、L和F基因表现出最高的变异性,并且没有检测到已知会降低尼塞韦单抗功效的突变。
解释与创新
RSV-A 多样性的增加是由于 突变 在 基因组 中分布更广泛,包括高频和低频变体,这突显了随着预防计划的扩大,持续使用 基因组监测 来检测潜在逃逸变体的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 尼塞韦单抗 实施下 RSV 进化 的 监测 策略提供了信息,强调需要监测可能影响预防效果的少数变异。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏接受 尼塞韦单抗 治疗的患者,限制了对逃逸变异的直接评估,但由于强大的测序和生物信息学分析,该研究为 RSV 基因组多样性和少数变异动态提供了高可信度的证据。

Genomic Evolution and Minority Variant Dynamics of RSV during the First Season of Nirsevimab Implementation in Italy (2024-2025)

Authors: Greta Romano, Roberta Campagna, Guglielmo Ferrari, Antonino Maria Guglielmo Pitrolo, Ombretta Turriziani, Matteo Fracella, Eleonora Coratti, Lilia Cinti, Piergiorgio Roberto, Petrarca Laura
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a leading cause of hospitalization despite the recent implementation of infant immunoprophylaxis programs. The inter- and intra-host level of genetic variation across the genome has not been extensively investigated. Hence, the aim of this study was to characterize the whole genome of RSV strains circulating within two Italian areas during the 2024-2025 RSV season, corresponding to the first season after implementation of the national nirsevimab prophylaxis program. Whole genome sequencing (WGS) was performed on 88 respiratory samples that tested positive for RSV. Clinical records showed that none of the sequenced patients had received nirsevimab prophylaxis. The WGS strains obtained in the present study were correlated with the RSV strains circulating in Europe using phylogenetic inference. A newly designed bioinformatic pipeline was developed to evaluate the extent of genetic variation within hosts and reveal the distribution of minority mutations among RSV lineages. RSV-A (n=54) showed significantly higher levels of nucleotide diversity than RSV-B (n=34). Among the RSV-A strains, lineage A.D.3 was the most prevalent (35/54, 64%), followed by A.D.1 (18/54, 33%). The A.D.5 lineage was identified in only one strain, while all 34 RSV-B strains were found to be lineage B.D.E. Analysis of variants across the major lineages (A.D.1, A.D.3 and B.D.E) revealed heterogeneous mutation patterns. The G, L and F genes showed the highest variability. Minority viral variants (0.02-0.05 frequency) followed the same gene-specific dynamics as the broader quasispecies population. A>G transitions consistent with ADAR activity were detected in all samples but their frequency was similar to the other nucleotide substitutions across the genomes. The analysis of minor variants also targeted mutations known to reduce nirsevimab efficacy, and none were detected. Overall, RSV-A showed substantially greater intra-genotypic and intra-host diversity than RSV-B. The elevated diversity in RSV-A resulted from a wider distribution of mutations across the viral genome, including both high-frequency substitutions that shaped the consensus sequence and low-frequency variants contributing to the underlying quasispecies complexity. Although no nirsevimab-treated patients were included in this study, the observed intra-host diversity highlights the importance of continued genomic surveillance to detect potential escape variants as prophylactic programs expand.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the whole genome of RSV strains circulating in two Italian areas during the 2024-2025 RSV season, the first after implementation of the national nirsevimab prophylaxis program, due to limited investigation of genetic variation at inter- and intra-host levels.
Design and population
Whole genome sequencing was performed on 88 respiratory samples that tested positive for RSV from patients who had not received nirsevimab prophylaxis.
Core methods
A newly designed bioinformatic pipeline was developed to evaluate genetic variation within hosts and reveal minority mutation distribution among RSV lineages, with phylogenetic inference to correlate strains with European circulation.
Main results
RSV-A (n=54) showed significantly higher nucleotide diversity than RSV-B (n=34), with A.D.3 being the most prevalent RSV-A lineage (64%), while all RSV-B strains were B.D.E.; the G, L and F genes showed the highest variability, and no mutations known to reduce nirsevimab efficacy were detected.
Interpretation and novelty
The elevated diversity in RSV-A resulted from a wider distribution of mutations across the genome, including both high-frequency and low-frequency variants, highlighting the importance of continued genomic surveillance to detect potential escape variants as prophylactic programs expand.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance strategies for RSV evolution under nirsevimab implementation, emphasizing the need to monitor minority variants that could impact prophylaxis efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The absence of nirsevimab-treated patients limits direct assessment of escape variants, but the study provides high-confidence evidence on RSV genomic diversity and minority variant dynamics due to robust sequencing and bioinformatic analysis.

波兰2025/2026季节住院患者病毒性呼吸道感染临床病程比较

Comparison of the Clinical Course of Viral Respiratory Infections in Hospitalized Patients During the 2025/2026 Season in Poland
作者： Piotr Rzymski, Małgorzata Wajdowicz, Szymon Piaszczyński, Piotr Czupryna, Karolina Turzańska, Monika Pazgan-simon, Paweł Skwara, Justyna Hlebowicz, Maciej Piaseck, Dorota Zarębska‐Michaluk

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 2025/2026 年季节波兰住院成人的病毒性呼吸道感染的临床病程,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),因为当代比较数据有限。
设计与对象
对 2025 年 9 月至 2026 年 4 月期间因实验室确诊的 RSV、流感或 COVID-19 感染而住院的连续成年人进行了一项回顾性多中心研究。大多数住院患者是老年人且未接种疫苗。
核心方法
分析了人口统计学特征、合并症、疫苗接种 状态、临床特征、实验室检查结果和结果。使用多变量逻辑回归确定了院内 死亡率 的独立预测因素。
主要结果
The study included 604 patients: 255 with COVID-19, 314 with influenza, and 35 with RSV infection. RSV patients were older, more frequently affected by multimorbidity, ischemic heart disease, and cancer, and showed the greatest respiratory impairment, including the highest rates of hypoxemia and pneumonia.
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 RSV 与其他呼吸道病毒相比的独特临床特征,强调了其对老年人呼吸损伤和合并症的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了有针对性的 监测、改进的老年人 疫苗接种 策略以及加强医院对 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病的准备的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于仅抽象的证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,这些数据对 RSV 对住院成人的临床影响进行了中等评估。

Comparison of the Clinical Course of Viral Respiratory Infections in Hospitalized Patients During the 2025/2026 Season in Poland

Authors: Piotr Rzymski, Małgorzata Wajdowicz, Szymon Piaszczyński, Piotr Czupryna, Karolina Turzańska, Monika Pazgan-simon, Paweł Skwara, Justyna Hlebowicz, Maciej Piaseck, Dorota Zarębska‐Michaluk
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: SARS-CoV-2, influenza viruses, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remain major causes of adult hospitalizations, but contemporary comparative data are limited. This study compared the epidemiological characteristics, clinical presentation, and outcomes of adults hospitalized with these infections during the 2025/2026 epidemic season in Poland. Methods: We conducted a retrospective multicenter study of consecutive adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed COVID-19, influenza, or RSV infection between September 2025 and April 2026. Demographic characteristics, comorbidities, vaccination status, clinical features, laboratory findings, and outcomes were analyzed. Independent predictors of in-hospital mortality were identified using multivariable logistic regression. Results: The study included 604 patients: 255 with COVID-19, 314 with influenza, and 35 with RSV infection. Distinct seasonal patterns were observed, with COVID-19 peaking in autumn, influenza in winter, and RSV in early spring. Most hospitalized patients were elderly and unvaccinated. RSV patients were older, more frequently affected by multimorbidity, ischemic heart disease, and cancer, and showed the greatest respiratory impairment, including the highest rates of hypoxemia and pneumonia. Influenza was characterized by more frequent fever, headache, and myalgia. Despite lower pneumonia rates, COVID-19 was associated with the highest in-hospital mortality (13.7%) and remained an independent predictor of death (aOR = 3.23, 95%CI: 1.68–6.22). Antibiotic use was common across all cohorts (62–77%). Conclusions: COVID-19 remained associated with the highest mortality among hospitalized adults, whereas RSV contributed substantially to respiratory morbidity in older individuals. These findings support improved vaccination uptake, continued surveillance, hospital preparedness, and antimicrobial stewardship.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the clinical course of viral respiratory infections, specifically focusing on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), in hospitalized adults during the 2025/2026 season in Poland, as contemporary comparative data are limited.
Design and population
A retrospective multicenter study was conducted on consecutive adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV, influenza, or COVID-19 infections between September 2025 and April 2026. Most hospitalized patients were elderly and unvaccinated.
Core methods
Demographic characteristics, comorbidities, vaccination status, clinical features, laboratory findings, and outcomes were analyzed. Independent predictors of in-hospital mortality were identified using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
The study included 604 patients: 255 with COVID-19, 314 with influenza, and 35 with RSV infection. RSV patients were older, more frequently affected by multimorbidity, ischemic heart disease, and cancer, and showed the greatest respiratory impairment, including the highest rates of hypoxemia and pneumonia.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the distinct clinical profiles of RSV compared to other respiratory viruses, emphasizing its impact on respiratory impairment and comorbidities in older adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results underscore the importance of targeted surveillance, improved vaccination strategies for older adults, and enhanced hospital preparedness for RSV-related respiratory morbidity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the data provide a moderate assessment of RSV's clinical impact in hospitalized adults.

基于动态调节的稳定 突变 对于设计 RSV 融合前 F mRNA 疫苗 非常有效

Dynamic regulation-based stabilizing mutations are highly effective for designing RSV pre-fusion F mRNA vaccines
作者： Shuai Shao, Ze Yuan Dong, Zi Yao Kang, Hao Zhang, Xiangshan Zheng, Fu Jie Shen, Yu Qin Jin, Zhengqun Pan, Xin Ge Li, Ning Liu
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是呼吸道感染的主要原因,对幼儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的人构成严重威胁。我们之前使用动态调节机制设计了融合前稳定的 F 蛋白 (pre-F) TriM-5,并生产了基于重组蛋白的 疫苗 候选物,该候选物表现出针对 RSV 的有效保护作用。然而,多项研究表明,以蛋白质形式实现的前 F 稳定性可能不足以应用于 mRNA 疫苗 开发。在这里,基于 TriM-5,我们设计了 mRNA 疫苗 候选者,以评估 TriM-5 的基于动态调节的稳定 突变 在 mRNA 格式中是否也有效。TriM-5 mRNA 使用名为 NVSI_GAmRNAopt 的内部软件进行密码子优化,该软件采用遗传算法,结合多个经验参数进行编码序列 (CDS) 优化,并使用脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 进行封装。小鼠免疫显示,CDS 优化后,IgG 抗体滴度增加了约三倍,表明我们的密码子优化算法的有效性。与我们专有的 BM028 LNP 封装的 Moderna RSV mRNA 序列相比,TriM-5 mRNA-LNP 诱导的 RSV A2 病毒中和抗体滴度高出 3.03 倍,并且 IFN-γ、IL-5 和 IL-17A 分泌水平显着更高,表明其具有优异的免疫原性。病毒攻击实验表明,TriM-5 mRNA-LNP 和 Moderna mRNA-LNP 均能有效降低肺部病毒载量,且未出现明显的肺部病理变化。设计的TriM-5 mRNA-LNP表现出良好的免疫原性和保护功效,因此可以作为进一步开发疫苗的有希望的候选者。

设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。

我们之前使用动态调节机制设计了融合前稳定的 F 蛋白 (pre-F) TriM-5,并生产了基于重组蛋白的 疫苗 候选物,该候选物表现出针对 RSV 的有效保护作用。
主要结果
病毒攻击实验表明,TriM-5 mRNA-LNP 和 Moderna mRNA-LNP 均能有效降低肺部病毒载量,且未出现明显的肺部病理变化。

与我们专有的 BM028 LNP 封装的 Moderna RSV mRNA 序列相比,TriM-5 mRNA-LNP 诱导的 RSV A2 病毒中和抗体滴度高出 3.03 倍,并且 IFN-γ、IL-5 和 IL-17A 分泌水平显着更高,表明其具有优异的免疫原性。

这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Dynamic regulation-based stabilizing mutations are highly effective for designing RSV pre-fusion F mRNA vaccines

Authors: Shuai Shao, Ze Yuan Dong, Zi Yao Kang, Hao Zhang, Xiangshan Zheng, Fu Jie Shen, Yu Qin Jin, Zhengqun Pan, Xin Ge Li, Ning Liu
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of respiratory tract infections and poses a serious threat to young children, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. We previously designed a pre-fusion stabilized F protein (pre-F), TriM-5, using a dynamic regulation mechanism, and produced a recombinant protein-based vaccine candidate that exhibited potent protection against RSV. However, several studies have revealed that the stabilization of pre-F achieved in protein form may not be sufficient for application in mRNA vaccine development. Here, based on TriM-5, we designed an mRNA vaccine candidate to assess whether the dynamic regulation-based stabilizing mutations of TriM-5 are also effective in an mRNA format. The TriM-5 mRNA was codon-optimized using in-house software named NVSI_GAmRNAopt, which employed a genetic algorithm incorporating multiple empirical parameters for coding sequence (CDS) optimization, and encapsulated using lipid nanoparticles (LNPs). Mouse immunization showed that after CDS optimization, the IgG antibody titers increased approximately threefold, indicating the effectiveness of our codon-optimization algorithm. Compared with the Moderna RSV mRNA sequence encapsulated with our proprietary BM028 LNPs, the TriM-5 mRNA-LNP induced 3.03-fold higher neutralizing antibody titers against RSV A2 virus, along with significantly higher IFN-γ, IL-5 and IL-17A secretion levels, indicating its superior immunogenicity. Virus challenge experiments demonstrated that both TriM-5 mRNA-LNP and Moderna mRNA-LNP reduced lung viral loads effectively, with no obvious lung pathology. The designed TriM-5 mRNA-LNP exhibits good immunogenicity and protective efficacy, and thereby may serve as a promising candidate for further vaccine development.

Core methods
We previously designed a pre-fusion stabilized F protein (pre-F), TriM-5, using a dynamic regulation mechanism, and produced a recombinant protein-based vaccine candidate that exhibited potent protection against RSV.
Main results
Virus challenge experiments demonstrated that both TriM-5 mRNA-LNP and Moderna mRNA-LNP reduced lung viral loads effectively, with no obvious lung pathology.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

定义 RSV 小疏水蛋白在病毒中的作用 发病机制 2255472

Defining the Role of the RSV Small Hydrophobic Protein in Viral Pathogenesis 2255472
作者： Ashlyn Bugbee, Steven Varga
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) accounts for 33 million infections worldwide each year and is the leading cause of lower respiratory tract infections in infants. The RSV small hydrophobic (SH) protein has been previously implicated in regulating the secretion of IL-1β following infection, but its role in modulating RSV disease severity beyond IL-1β production remains unknown.
设计与对象
该研究利用第 63 位氨基酸编码天冬氨酸的重组 A2 实验室毒株病毒 (A2 SH N63D) 来研究 N64 突变 的影响。实验在小鼠和人单核细胞 THP-1 细胞系中进行,具有 NF-κB 和 I 型 IFN 反应的双报告基因。
核心方法
使用噬菌斑测定、ELISA、胞质 mtDNA 的 qPCR 以及具有 NF-κB 和 I 型 IFN 反应双报告基因的人单核细胞 THP-1 细胞系来研究 N64 突变 的影响。生成 A2 SH N63D 突变体并与野生型 A2 (WT A2) 进行比较。
主要结果
与野生型 A2 相比,A2 SH N63D 突变体在小鼠体内引起病毒滴度降低和早期疾病。A2 SH N63D 感染后观察到炎症细胞因子增加和 I 型 IFN 反应,以及 THP-1 细胞内 mtDNA 释放和 STING 依赖性 I 型 IFN 反应。
解释与创新
结果表明,SH 蛋白 C 末端的天冬氨酸可能有助于 RSV 发病机制 和 I 型 IFN 反应。这一发现很新颖,因为它扩展了对 SH 蛋白在 IL-1β 调节之外的作用的理解。
科研与公卫意义
通过关注 SH 蛋白在 发病机制 中的作用,这些发现可能为针对 RSV 的靶向疗法的开发提供信息。了解 SH 蛋白对疾病严重程度的影响可能会改进 监测 和 RSV 感染的干预策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在小鼠和 THP-1 细胞系中进行的,这可能无法完全重现人类 RSV 感染。由于研究范围有限且使用实验室菌株而非临床分离株,证据强度中等。

Defining the Role of the RSV Small Hydrophobic Protein in Viral Pathogenesis 2255472

Authors: Ashlyn Bugbee, Steven Varga
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) accounts for 33 million infections worldwide each year and is the leading cause of lower respiratory tract infections in infants. Severe RSV-induced disease is associated with the host response to the infection including the increased production of the inflammatory cytokines IL-1β and IL-6. The RSV small hydrophobic (SH) protein has been previously implicated in regulating the secretion of IL-1β following infection. The SH protein is classified as a viroporin, a class of transmembrane proteins that oligomerize to form pores in membranes and can facilitate ion flux to modulate multiple signaling pathways. It is currently unknown what role SH may play in modulating RSV disease severity beyond the modulation of IL-1β production; however, in 2017 there was an outbreak of RSV-B cases containing a mutation in the SH protein that resulted in high hospitalization rates. This outbreak occurred in the summer, an unusual season for RSV cases prior to the COVID-19 pandemic, with the virus expressing a single nucleotide polymorphism within the SH protein that changed an asparagine into an aspartic acid at amino acid 64 (SH N64D). Methods To study the impact of the N64 mutation, we generated a recombinant A2 laboratory strain virus encoded with an aspartic acid at amino acid 63 which is termed A2 SH N63D. We utilized plaque assays, ELISAs, qPCR for cytosolic mtDNA, and human monocyte THP-1 cell lines with a dual reporter for NF-κB and type I IFN response. Results The A2 SH N63D mutant elicited decreased viral titers and early disease compared to the wildtype A2 (WT A2) in mice. We have also observed increased inflammatory cytokines and a type I IFN response following A2 SH N63D infection. We have observed mtDNA release after A2 SH N63D infection within THP-1 cells as well as a STING-dependent type I IFN response Conclusion These results suggest that the aspartic acid within the C-terminus of the SH protein may contribute to RSV pathogenesis and the type I IFN response. Funding Source NIAID R01 AI167249 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) accounts for 33 million infections worldwide each year and is the leading cause of lower respiratory tract infections in infants. The RSV small hydrophobic (SH) protein has been previously implicated in regulating the secretion of IL-1β following infection, but its role in modulating RSV disease severity beyond IL-1β production remains unknown.
Design and population
The study utilized a recombinant A2 laboratory strain virus encoded with an aspartic acid at amino acid 63 (A2 SH N63D) to investigate the impact of the N64 mutation. Experiments were conducted in mice and human monocyte THP-1 cell lines with a dual reporter for NF-κB and type I IFN response.
Core methods
Plaque assays, ELISAs, qPCR for cytosolic mtDNA, and human monocyte THP-1 cell lines with a dual reporter for NF-κB and type I IFN response were used to study the impact of the N64 mutation. The A2 SH N63D mutant was generated and compared to the wildtype A2 (WT A2).
Main results
The A2 SH N63D mutant elicited decreased viral titers and early disease compared to the wildtype A2 in mice. Increased inflammatory cytokines and a type I IFN response were observed following A2 SH N63D infection, along with mtDNA release within THP-1 cells and a STING-dependent type I IFN response.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that the aspartic acid within the C-terminus of the SH protein may contribute to RSV pathogenesis and the type I IFN response. This finding is novel as it expands the understanding of the SH protein's role beyond IL-1β modulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted therapies against RSV by focusing on the SH protein's role in pathogenesis. Understanding the SH protein's impact on disease severity could lead to improved surveillance and intervention strategies for RSV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in mice and THP-1 cell lines, which may not fully recapitulate human RSV infection. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of the study and the use of laboratory strains rather than clinical isolates.

记忆 CD4+ T 细胞中的 GLUT1 与免疫功能低下个体中 RSV 疫苗接种 后的持续抗体反应和多功能性相关 2250589

GLUT1 in Memory CD4+ T-cells Correlates with Sustained Antibody Responses and Polyfunctionality Post-RSV Vaccination in Immunocompromised Individuals 2250589
作者： Nirvani Jairam, Zeba Nauroz, Maggie Chahoud, Xori Green, Woudase Gallo, Prasanthy Balsubramain, Isabella Sengsouk, William Werbel, Andrew Karaba, Andrea Cox
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 由于该群体中已发表的数据有限,特别是关于新型 RSV 疫苗 的细胞反应的数据有限,因此对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 在免疫功能低下个体中的免疫原性知之甚少。为了解决这一差距,我们对接受批准的 RSV 预融合 F (preF) 蛋白亚基 疫苗 的免疫功能低下个体的 T 细胞反应进行了评估。方法 在一项全国性前瞻性观察 队列研究 中,44 名免疫功能低下个体报告接受了有佐剂的 RSVPreF3 (AREXVYTM) 或无佐剂的 RSVpreF (ABRYSVOTM)。队列研究 主要是实体器官移植受者,但也包括因服用免疫抑制剂而患有自身免疫性疾病的参与者。在 RSV-A2 融合蛋白肽池刺激、ELISA 和活病毒中和后,分别在基线、疫苗接种 后早期(2 或 4 周)和晚期(12 周)使用流式细胞术评估 T 细胞反应、RSV preF 抗体和 RSV 中和抗体。结果 疫苗接种 后,产生 IFN-γ、IL-2 和 TNF 的 RSV 特异性记忆 CD4+ T 细胞显着增加,并且多功能 CD4+ T 细胞也增加。两种 疫苗 均增加了 CD4+ T 细胞的多功能性,但没有显着差异。一部分个体的 CD4+ T 细胞反应没有增加,这表明需要研究反应的预测因素。GLUT1(葡萄糖转运蛋白 1)是 T 细胞代谢激活和健康的标志物。在产生细胞因子的记忆 CD4+ T 细胞中,GLUT1 表达与晚期时间点的多功能性、RSV F 特异性 IgG 滴度和中和抗体滴度 (NT50) 相关,但与早期时间点无关。结论 这些发现表明,如 GLUT1 所示,持续的 T 细胞代谢适应性可能与细胞和体液 免疫应答 的持久性有关。了解 GLUT1 在塑造 免疫应答 中的作用可能为优化免疫功能低下个体的 疫苗 策略提供新方法。资金来源 美国国立卫生研究院 (NIH) 拨款 1R01AI190359、3U01AI138897-04S1 和 3UM1AI109565 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于该群体中已发表的数据有限,特别是关于新型 RSV 疫苗 的细胞反应的数据有限,因此对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 在免疫功能低下个体中的免疫原性知之甚少。
设计与对象
在一项全国性前瞻性观察 队列研究 中,44 名免疫功能低下者报告接受了有佐剂的 RSVPreF3 (AREXVYTM) 或无佐剂的 RSVpreF (ABRYSVOTM)。队列研究 主要是实体器官移植受者,但也包括因服用免疫抑制剂而患有自身免疫性疾病的参与者。
核心方法
在 RSV-A2 融合蛋白肽池刺激、ELISA 和活病毒中和后,分别在基线、疫苗接种 后早期(2 或 4 周)和晚期(12 周)使用流式细胞术评估 T 细胞反应、RSV preF 抗体和 RSV 中和抗体。
主要结果
疫苗接种 后,产生 IFN-γ、IL-2 和 TNF 的 RSV 特异性记忆 CD4+ T 细胞显着增加,并且多功能 CD4+ T 细胞也增加。两种 疫苗 均增加了 CD4+ T 细胞的多功能性,但没有显着差异。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,如 GLUT1 所示,持续的 T 细胞代谢适应性可能与细胞和体液 免疫应答 的持久性有关。了解 GLUT1 在塑造 免疫应答 中的作用可能为优化免疫功能低下个体的 疫苗 策略提供新方法。
科研与公卫意义
该研究将 GLUT1 确定为一种潜在的生物标志物,用于预测免疫功能低下个体 RSV 疫苗接种 后持续抗体反应和多功能 T 细胞反应,这可以为个性化 疫苗 策略提供信息并改善这一弱势群体的结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,样本量较小,只有 44 名免疫功能低下的个体,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,多重测定和纵向评估的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

GLUT1 in Memory CD4+ T-cells Correlates with Sustained Antibody Responses and Polyfunctionality Post-RSV Vaccination in Immunocompromised Individuals 2250589

Authors: Nirvani Jairam, Zeba Nauroz, Maggie Chahoud, Xori Green, Woudase Gallo, Prasanthy Balsubramain, Isabella Sengsouk, William Werbel, Andrew Karaba, Andrea Cox
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction The immunogenicity of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination in immunocompromised individuals is poorly understood due to limited published data in this population, specifically on cellular responses to novel RSV vaccines. To address this gap, T-cell responses were evaluated in immunocompromised individuals who received an approved RSV prefusion F (preF) protein subunit vaccine. Methods In a national prospective observational cohort, 44 immunocompromised individuals reported receiving either adjuvanted RSVPreF3 (AREXVYTM) or unadjuvanted RSVpreF (ABRYSVOTM). The cohort was predominantly solid organ transplant recipients but also included participants with autoimmune conditions on immunosuppressants. T-cell responses, RSV preF antibodies, and RSV neutralizing antibodies were assessed at baseline, early (2 or 4 weeks), and late (12 weeks) post-vaccination using flow cytometry following RSV-A2 fusion protein peptide pool stimulation, ELISA, and live-virus neutralization, respectively. Results Post-vaccination, there were significant increases in RSV-specific memory CD4+ T-cells producing IFN-γ, IL-2, and TNF, and increases in polyfunctional CD4+ T-cells. Both vaccines, without significant differences, increased CD4+ T-cell polyfunctionality. A subset of individuals did not have an increase in CD4+ T-cell responses, demonstrating the need to investigate predictors of response. GLUT1 (Glucose transporter 1) is a marker of T-cell metabolic activation and fitness. In cytokine-producing memory CD4+ T-cells, GLUT1 expression correlated with polyfunctionality, RSV F-specific IgG titers, and neutralizing antibody titers (NT50) at the late but not the early time point. Conclusion These findings suggest that sustained T-cell metabolic fitness, as indicated by GLUT1, may be linked to the durability of both cellular and humoral immune responses. Understanding the role of GLUT1 in shaping immune responses may provide novel methods for optimizing vaccine strategies for immunocompromised individuals. Funding Source National Institutes of Health (NIH) grants 1R01AI190359, 3U01AI138897-04S1, and 3UM1AI109565 Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)

Question and background
The immunogenicity of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination in immunocompromised individuals is poorly understood due to limited published data in this population, specifically on cellular responses to novel RSV vaccines.
Design and population
In a national prospective observational cohort, 44 immunocompromised individuals reported receiving either adjuvanted RSVPreF3 (AREXVYTM) or unadjuvanted RSVpreF (ABRYSVOTM). The cohort was predominantly solid organ transplant recipients but also included participants with autoimmune conditions on immunosuppressants.
Core methods

使用 QSAR 建模、分子对接和分子动力学模拟计算鉴定潜在的 RSV L-RdRp 抑制剂,预计其活性和安全性优于瑞德西韦

Computational Identification of Potential RSV L-RdRp Inhibitors with Predicted Superior Activity and Safety Profiles over Remdesivir Using QSAR Modeling, Molecular Docking and Molecular Dynamics Simulations
作者： Yini Xie, Runqing Jia, Shuo Chen, Feng Li, Guohui Sun, 贾润清
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶 (RdRp) 复合物是病毒 基因组 复制的重要分子机器。L 蛋白是该复合物 (L-RdRp) 的催化亚基,其结构特征良好,是开发新型抗 RSV 小分子药物的一个非常有前景的靶点。方法:为了解决当前基于 QSAR 的 RSV L-RdRp 抑制剂开发虚拟筛选策略的局限性,我们建立了一个集活性、毒性、药物相似性和稳定性于一体的多维计算机辅助药物筛选框架。结果:开发了两个符合 OECD 要求的 2D-QSAR 模型并经过严格验证,分别用于预测抑制活性和细胞毒性。最佳抑制活性模型表现出较强的统计性能,R2=0.8281,QLOO2=0.7653,Rtest2=0.8713,QFn2=0.8594∼0.8837,CCCtest=0.9301,MAEtest=0.1966。同样,最佳细胞毒性模型达到R2= 0.8263,QLOO2 = 0.7422,Rtest2 = 0.8951,QFn2 = 0.8108~0.8530,CCCtest = 0.9081,MAEtest = 0.1685。基于这些模型,实施了四步筛选工作流程——基于 QSAR 的过滤和分子对接(15,758 → 2446 → 162 → 19 种化合物)、ADMET 评估(19 → 5)和分子动力学模拟(MDS)——以识别有前景的 L-RdRp 抑制剂。结论:最终筛选出5个候选化合物,与参比药瑞德西韦相比,所有候选化合物均具有较高的抑制活性、较低的细胞毒性、稳定的预测结合模式和良好的口服生物利用度。这些发现为硅衍生的先导候选物提供了有价值的信息,并为识别针对 RSV 的实验性 L-RdRp 抑制剂提供了可靠的计算工作流程。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) L-RdRp 复合物的新型小分子抑制剂,由于当前虚拟筛选策略的局限性,该复合物是一个有前途但尚未充分开发的药物靶点。
设计与对象
这项计算研究采用了多维计算机辅助药物筛选框架,综合了活性、毒性、药物相似性和稳定性来识别潜在的 RSV L-RdRp 抑制剂。
核心方法
研究人员开发了两个符合 OECD 要求的 2D-QSAR 模型来预测抑制活性和细胞毒性,然后是四步筛选工作流程,包括基于 QSAR 的过滤、分子对接、ADMET 评估和分子动力学模拟。
主要结果
最佳抑制活性模型表现出很强的统计性能(R2 = 0.8281,QLOO2 = 0.7653,Rtest2 = 0.8713),而细胞毒性模型则达到了R2 = 0.8263,Rtest2 = 0.8951;最终,选择了五种候选化合物,预计其活性比瑞德西韦更高,细胞毒性更低。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为提供了有价值的硅衍生先导候选物和可靠的计算工作流程,用于识别针对 RSV 的实验性 L-RdRp 抑制剂,代表了针对该病毒的药物发现的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些计算结果可以为开发更有效、更安全的 RSV 疗法提供信息,从而有可能改善 RSV 感染的 治疗 选择,而 RSV 感染仍然是一个重大的公共卫生负担。
局限与证据强度
尽管 QSAR 模型的严格验证和全面的筛选工作流程支持了计算机研究结果的可靠性,但证据强度适中,因为该研究完全依赖于计算预测而没有实验验证。

Computational Identification of Potential RSV L-RdRp Inhibitors with Predicted Superior Activity and Safety Profiles over Remdesivir Using QSAR Modeling, Molecular Docking and Molecular Dynamics Simulations

Authors: Yini Xie, Runqing Jia, Shuo Chen, Feng Li, Guohui Sun, 贾润清
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp) complex is an essential molecular machine for viral genome replication. The L protein, the catalytic subunit of this complex (L-RdRp), is well-characterized structurally and represents a highly promising target for the development of novel small-molecule drugs against RSV. Methods: To address the limitations of current QSAR-based virtual screening strategies for RSV L-RdRp inhibitor development, we established a multi-dimensional computer-aided drug screening framework integrating activity, toxicity, drug-likeness, and stability. Results: Two OECD-compliant 2D-QSAR models were developed and rigorously validated to predict inhibitory activity and cytotoxicity, respectively. The optimal inhibitory activity model exhibited strong statistical performance, with R2 = 0.8281, QLOO2= 0.7653, Rtest2= 0.8713, QFn2= 0.8594 ∼ 0.8837, CCCtest = 0.9301, MAEtest = 0.1966. Similarly, the best cytotoxicity model achieved R2= 0.8263, QLOO2 = 0.7422, Rtest2 = 0.8951, QFn2 = 0.8108~0.8530, CCCtest = 0.9081, MAEtest = 0.1685. Based on these models, a four-step screening workflow—QSAR-based filtering and molecular docking (15,758 → 2446 → 162 → 19 compounds), ADMET evaluation (19 → 5), and molecular dynamics simulations (MDSs)—was implemented to identify promising L-RdRp inhibitors. Conclusions: Ultimately, five candidate compounds were selected, all of which demonstrated predicted higher inhibitory activity, lower predicted cytotoxicity, a stable predicted binding mode, and favorable oral bioavailability compared with the reference drug remdesivir. These findings provide valuable in silico-derived lead candidates and a reliable computational workflow for identifying experimental L-RdRp inhibitors targeting RSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify novel small-molecule inhibitors targeting the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) L-RdRp complex, a promising but underexploited drug target, due to the limitations of current virtual screening strategies.
Design and population
This computational study employed a multi-dimensional computer-aided drug screening framework integrating activity, toxicity, drug-likeness, and stability to identify potential RSV L-RdRp inhibitors.
Core methods
The researchers developed two OECD-compliant 2D-QSAR models to predict inhibitory activity and cytotoxicity, followed by a four-step screening workflow including QSAR-based filtering, molecular docking, ADMET evaluation, and molecular dynamics simulations.
Main results
The optimal inhibitory activity model showed strong statistical performance (R2 = 0.8281, QLOO2 = 0.7653, Rtest2 = 0.8713), while the cytotoxicity model achieved R2 = 0.8263, Rtest2 = 0.8951; ultimately, five candidate compounds were selected with predicted higher activity and lower cytotoxicity than remdesivir.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as providing valuable in silico-derived lead candidates and a reliable computational workflow for identifying experimental L-RdRp inhibitors targeting RSV, representing a novel approach to drug discovery for this virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These computational results could inform the development of more effective and safer RSV therapeutics, potentially improving treatment options for RSV infections, which remain a significant public health burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as this study relied entirely on computational predictions without experimental validation, though the rigorous validation of QSAR models and comprehensive screening workflow support the reliability of the in silico findings.

Neuregulin-1 防止 RSV 引起的人气道上皮极性破坏 2257854

Neuregulin-1 Prevents RSV Induced Disruption of Human Airway Epithelium Polarity 2257854
作者： Syed-Rehan Hussain, Phylip Chen, Matthew Bochter, Michell Rohlfing, Mark Peeples, Mitchell Grayson
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 呼吸道病毒在幼儿和老年人中引起显着的发病率和 死亡率。死亡率 对呼吸道病毒的作用机制尚不清楚。我们研究了 Neuregulin-1 (NRG1),它是 ERBb 受体酪氨酸激酶 (ERBb2 -4) 的配体,发现带有 NRG1 的 治疗 前体可以为感染正常致死剂量的甲型流感病毒或仙台病毒(一种啮齿动物副流感病毒)的小鼠提供约 75% 的存活率。向人支气管上皮细胞 (hBEC) 施用 NRG1 可增加 RSV 感染后的上皮增殖和修复。上皮屏障功能与上皮极性密切相关;极化上皮细胞具有通过 3 个极性复合体维持的顶端-基底轴:顶端的 PAR (Par3/Par6/aPKC) 和 CRB (Crumbs/Pals/Patj),以及基底外侧的 SCRIB (Scrib/Dlg1/Lgl)。活性氧 (ROS) 在气道上皮通透性中发挥作用,也会影响极性。目前的研究确定了在 hBEC 感染 RSV 之前预防性给予 NRG1 对极性的影响。方法 将在 Transwell 中生长的 hBEC 用人 NRG1 (100 ng) 在基侧预处理 5 天,然后将 RSV-gfp (4000 pfu) 添加到顶端侧。将抗 ERBb2 (10 mcg) 和抗 ERBb3 (0.1 mcg) 抗体添加到基底侧 5 天。感染后第 5 天对石蜡切片进行免疫组织化学检查极性标记(CRB3 和 DLG1)。此外,hBEC 在 CO2 培养箱中于 37oC 下用 MitoSOX 超氧化物指示剂处理 30 分钟,以检测活细胞中线粒体超氧化物的产生。结果 NRG1 阻止了 RSV 对 CRB3 定位的破坏,这一作用被抗 ERBb3 阻断。阻断 ERBb2 会减少上皮增殖(ERBb3 拮抗作用则不会),但不会影响极性蛋白的定位。NRG1 治疗 减少 RSV 感染细胞中的 mitoROS。结论 NRG1 可以防止气道上皮顶端-基底极性的破坏,并减少过量的 ROS,从而在感染期间稳定 hBEC。资金来源 这项工作得到了美国国立卫生研究院 (NIH) 拨款 R01AI171027(授予 Mitchell H Grayson (MHG))的支持;R01 AI093848 和 U19AI42733(致 Mark E Peeples)),罗伯特·F·沃尔夫和埃德加·T·沃尔夫基金会(致 MHG)。主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒在幼儿和老年人中引起显着的发病率和 死亡率,但 死亡率 的机制尚不清楚。本研究调查了 Neuregulin-1 (NRG1) 在 RSV 感染期间维持气道上皮极性的作用。
设计与对象
该研究使用在 RSV 感染前用 NRG1 预处理的 Transwell 中生长的人支气管上皮细胞 (hBEC)。用抗ERBb2和抗ERBb3抗体处理细胞,并对极性标记进行免疫组织化学分析。
核心方法
在 RSV-gfp 感染前,用人 NRG1 (100 ng) 预处理 hBEC 5 天。对 CRB3 和 DLG1 标记物进行免疫组织化学分析,并使用 MitoSOX 指示剂检测线粒体超氧化物的产生。
主要结果
NRG1 阻止了 RSV 对 CRB3 定位的破坏,这种作用被抗 ERBb3 阻断。NRG1 治疗 还减少了 RSV 感染细胞中的 mitoROS。
解释与创新
作者解释说,NRG1 通过防止顶端基底极性破坏和减少 RSV 感染期间过量的 ROS 来稳定气道上皮。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,NRG1 可能是一种候选治疗药物,可在 RSV 感染期间保护气道上皮完整性,从而有可能降低疾病的严重程度。
局限与证据强度
该研究是使用 hBEC 在体外进行的,限制了体内条件的普遍性。由于实验室环境受控,证据强度中等,但有具体机制研究结果支持。

Neuregulin-1 Prevents RSV Induced Disruption of Human Airway Epithelium Polarity 2257854

Authors: Syed-Rehan Hussain, Phylip Chen, Matthew Bochter, Michell Rohlfing, Mark Peeples, Mitchell Grayson
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Respiratory viruses cause significant morbidity and mortality in very young and old. Mechanism of mortality to respiratory viruses are unknown. We have studied Neuregulin-1 (NRG1), a ligand for ERBb receptor tyrosine kinases (ERBb2 -4) and found pre-treatment with NRG1 provides ∼75% survival for mice infected with normally lethal doses of influenza A or Sendai virus (a rodent para-influenza virus). Administering NRG1 to human bronchial epithelial cells (hBEC) increased epithelial proliferation and repair following infection with RSV. Epithelial barrier function closely links to epithelial polarity; polarized epithelial cells have an apico-basal axis maintained through 3 polarity complexes: PAR (Par3/Par6/aPKC) and CRB (Crumbs/Pals/Patj), apically, and SCRIB (Scrib/Dlg1/Lgl), basolaterally. Reactive oxygen species (ROS) play a role in airway epithelium permeability also affecting polarity. The current study determined the effect of NRG1 on polarity when given prophylactically before RSV infection of hBEC. Methods hBEC grown in transwell were pretreated on the basal side for 5 days with human NRG1 (100 ng) before RSV-gfp (4000 pfu) was added to the apical side. Anti-ERBb2 (10mcg) and anti-ERBb3 (0.1 mcg) antibodies were added for 5 days to the basal side. Immunohistochemistry was performed on paraffin sections for polarity markers (CRB3 and DLG1) d5 post infection. In addition, hBEC were treated for 30 mins at 37oC in CO2 incubator with MitoSOX superoxide indicators to detect mitochondrial superoxide production in live cells. Results NRG1 prevented disruption of CRB3 localization by RSV, an effect blocked by anti-ERBb3. Blocking ERBb2 reduced epithelial proliferation (something ERBb3 antagonism did not do), but did not affect localization of polarity proteins. NRG1 treatment reduced mitoROS in RSV infected cells. Conclusion NRG1 prevents disruption of airway epithelium apico-basal polarity and reduces excessive ROS thus stabilizing hBEC during infection. Funding Source This work was supported by National Institutes of Health (NIH) grants R01AI171027 (to Mitchell H Grayson (MHG)); R01 AI093848 and U19AI42733 (to Mark E Peeples)), the Robert F Wolfe & Edgar T Wolfe Foundation (to MHG). Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses cause significant morbidity and mortality in very young and old, but the mechanisms of mortality are unknown. This study investigated the effect of Neuregulin-1 (NRG1) on maintaining airway epithelium polarity during RSV infection.
Design and population
The study used human bronchial epithelial cells (hBEC) grown in transwell pretreated with NRG1 before RSV infection. Cells were treated with anti-ERBb2 and anti-ERBb3 antibodies, and immunohistochemistry was performed for polarity markers.
Core methods
hBEC were pretreated with human NRG1 (100 ng) for 5 days before RSV-gfp infection. Immunohistochemistry was performed for CRB3 and DLG1 markers, and mitochondrial superoxide production was detected using MitoSOX indicators.
Main results
NRG1 prevented disruption of CRB3 localization by RSV, an effect blocked by anti-ERBb3. NRG1 treatment also reduced mitoROS in RSV-infected cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that NRG1 stabilizes airway epithelium by preventing disruption of apico-basal polarity and reducing excessive ROS during RSV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest NRG1 could be a therapeutic candidate to protect airway epithelial integrity during RSV infection, potentially reducing disease severity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro using hBEC, limiting generalizability to in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the controlled laboratory setting but supported by specific mechanistic findings.

用于无标记且多重兼容的呼吸道病毒检测的多功能亚波长光栅微环谐振器

Versatile Subwavelength Grating Microring Resonators for Label-Free and Multiplex-Compatible Detection of Respiratory Viruses
作者： Wanxin LI, Li Rui, Jiewen Li, Fatao Chen, Zheng CAO, Yongkang DONG, Yi ZOU, Xiaochuan Xu, Rui Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2)、甲型流感 (H1N1) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等呼吸道病毒由于临床症状重叠以及传统诊断方法的速度、灵敏度和多重检测能力有限,仍然是主要的公共卫生威胁。在这里,我们提出了一种基于亚波长光栅微环谐振器(SGMRR)的无标记光学生物传感平台,用于灵敏且特异性地检测病毒抗原。传感器表面带有针对三种代表性病毒蛋白的抗体,可通过抗原结合时的共振波长变化进行定量检测。该平台的检测折射率限 (LoD) 为 1.25×10−4 RIU,能够检测 100 pg/mL 的 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白、10 pg/mL 的 H1N1 核蛋白和 10 pg/mL 的 RSV F-三聚体蛋白。紧凑的 SGMRR 设计可轻松支持通过多环或多通道架构的多重集成,突显其作为可扩展的高性能平台的潜力,可用于快速、定量和即时病毒诊断。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于重叠的临床症状以及传统诊断方法的速度、灵敏度和多重能力有限,SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感 (H1N1) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等呼吸道病毒仍然是主要的公共卫生威胁。
设计与对象
本研究提出了一种基于亚波长光栅微环谐振器(SGMRR)的无标记光学生物传感平台,用于灵敏且特异性地检测病毒抗原。
核心方法
传感器表面带有针对三种代表性病毒蛋白的抗体,可通过抗原结合时的共振波长变化进行定量检测。
主要结果
该平台实现了 1.25×10−4 RIU 的折射率检测限 (LoD),并能够检测 100 pg/mL 的 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白、10 pg/mL 的 H1N1 核蛋白和 10 pg/mL 的 RSV F-三聚体蛋白。
解释与创新
紧凑的 SGMRR 设计可轻松支持通过多环或多通道架构的多重集成,突显其作为可扩展的高性能平台的潜力,可用于快速、定量和即时病毒诊断。
科研与公卫意义
该技术为改善呼吸道病毒(包括 RSV)的检测提供了一种有前途的解决方案,具有高灵敏度和多重功能,可以提高临床环境中的诊断准确性和速度。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于仅摘要的报告,可能缺乏详细的验证和实际测试;然而,报告的性能指标有力地证明了该平台的潜力。

Versatile Subwavelength Grating Microring Resonators for Label-Free and Multiplex-Compatible Detection of Respiratory Viruses

Authors: Wanxin LI, Li Rui, Jiewen Li, Fatao Chen, Zheng CAO, Yongkang DONG, Yi ZOU, Xiaochuan Xu, Rui Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory viruses, such as the severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), influenza A (H1N1), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), remain major public health threats due to overlapping clinical symptoms and the limited speed, sensitivity, and multiplexing capacity of conventional diagnostic methods. Here, we present a label-free optical biosensing platform based on subwavelength grating microring resonators (SGMRRs) for the sensitive and specific detection of viral antigens. The sensor surface is functionalized with antibodies against three representative viral proteins, enabling quantitative detection via resonance wavelength shifts upon antigen binding. The platform achieves the refractive index limit of detection (LoD) of 1.25×10−4 RIU and enables the detection of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein at 100 pg/mL, the H1N1 nucleoprotein at 10 pg/mL, and the RSV F-trimer protein at 10 pg/mL. The compact SGMRR design readily supports multiplexed integration through multi-ring or multi-channel architectures, highlighting its potential as a scalable and high-performance platform for rapid, quantitative, and point-of-care viral diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses, such as SARS-CoV-2, influenza A (H1N1), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), remain major public health threats due to overlapping clinical symptoms and the limited speed, sensitivity, and multiplexing capacity of conventional diagnostic methods.

Core methods

This technology offers a promising solution for improving the detection of respiratory viruses, including RSV, with high sensitivity and multiplexing capabilities, which could enhance diagnostic accuracy and speed in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only report, which may lack detailed validation and real-world testing; however, the reported performance metrics suggest strong evidence for the platform's potential.

唐氏综合症的免疫失调:对感染易感性和 疫苗 反应的影响

Immune Dysregulation in Down Syndrome: Implications for Infectious Susceptibility and Vaccine Response
作者： Sofía Solari-Hernández, Enrique González-Madrid, Pablo A. González, Susan M. Bueno, Alexis M. Kalergis
摘要中文翻译唐氏综合症 (DS) 由 21 三体引起,其特征是复杂的免疫失调,增加对感染的易感性并改变 疫苗 反应性。涉及 21 号染色体上干扰素受体基因座的基因剂量效应有助于慢性 I 型干扰素过度激活、持续的 JAK-STAT 信号传导以及干扰素刺激基因的持续表达。这种基线炎症状态伴随着先天性和适应性免疫的数量和功能缺陷,包括中性粒细胞趋化性受损、促炎性单核细胞极化、幼稚T细胞和B细胞区室减少、抗原受体多样性受限、类别转换记忆B细胞减少以及加速免疫衰老的特征。临床上,这些免疫改变与严重呼吸道病毒感染(例如 呼吸道合胞病毒、流感和 SARS-CoV-2)的风险增加以及 疫苗 诱导的免疫力的强度和持久性降低有关。队列研究 在儿童和成人人群中的研究报告了 队列研究 后住院率和 死亡率 较高,以及 疫苗接种 后峰值抗体滴度较低和免疫力更快减弱。本综述整合了分子、细胞和临床证据来定义 DS 中干扰素驱动的免疫表型及其对感染易感性和 疫苗接种 反应的影响。对这种免疫环境的机械理解为针对这一弱势群体制定量身定制的预防策略和优化 疫苗接种 方法提供了一个框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨唐氏综合症的免疫失调,这种失调会增加感染的易感性并影响 疫苗 反应,特别关注 呼吸道合胞病毒。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了 队列研究 在唐氏综合症儿童和成人人群中的研究,检查了免疫改变及其与严重呼吸道病毒感染和 疫苗 诱导的免疫力降低的关系。
核心方法
这些方法涉及整合现有 队列研究 研究中的分子、细胞和临床证据,以确定唐氏综合症中干扰素驱动的免疫表型及其对感染易感性和 疫苗接种 反应的影响。
主要结果
队列研究 研究报告称,唐氏综合症患者在接受 队列研究 治疗后,住院率和 死亡率 率较高,抗体滴度峰值较低,免疫力下降较快。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为唐氏综合症中由干扰素过度激活驱动的独特免疫表型的证据,这对于理解呼吸道病毒感染易感性增加背后的机制具有重要意义。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为唐氏综合症患者制定量身定制的预防策略和优化 疫苗接种 方法提供了一个框架,以解决其严重呼吸道病毒感染的高风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有的队列研究数据,没有呈现新的实验结果,限制了机理理解的深度。然而,多线证据的整合支持适度的置信度评估。

Immune Dysregulation in Down Syndrome: Implications for Infectious Susceptibility and Vaccine Response

Authors: Sofía Solari-Hernández, Enrique González-Madrid, Pablo A. González, Susan M. Bueno, Alexis M. Kalergis
Original abstract (English)
Down syndrome (DS), caused by trisomy 21, is characterized by complex immune dysregulation that increases susceptibility to infections and alters vaccine responsiveness. Gene dosage effects involving interferon receptor loci on chromosome 21 contribute to chronic type I interferon hyperactivation, sustained JAK-STAT signaling, and persistent expression of interferon-stimulated genes. This baseline inflammatory state is accompanied by quantitative and functional defects in both innate and adaptive immunity, including impaired neutrophil chemotaxis, pro-inflammatory monocyte polarization, reduced naïve T- and B-cell compartments, restricted antigen receptor diversity, diminished class-switched memory B cells, and features of accelerated immunosenescence. Clinically, these immune alterations are associated with an increased risk of severe respiratory viral infections, such as respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, and SARS-CoV-2, as well as reduced magnitude and durability of vaccine-induced immunity. Cohort studies in both pediatric and adult populations have reported higher rates of hospitalization and mortality, along with lower peak antibody titers and more rapid waning of immunity following vaccination. This review integrates molecular, cellular, and clinical evidence to define the interferon-driven immune phenotype in DS and its implications for susceptibility to infection and vaccination response. A mechanistic understanding of this immune landscape provides a framework for developing tailored preventive strategies and optimizing vaccination approaches in this vulnerable population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the immune dysregulation in Down syndrome that increases susceptibility to infections and affects vaccine response, particularly focusing on respiratory syncytial virus.
Design and population
The study is a review of cohort studies in both pediatric and adult populations with Down syndrome, examining immune alterations and their association with severe respiratory viral infections and reduced vaccine-induced immunity.
Core methods
The methods involved integrating molecular, cellular, and clinical evidence from existing cohort studies to define the interferon-driven immune phenotype in Down syndrome and its implications for susceptibility to infection and vaccination response.
Main results
Cohort studies reported higher rates of hospitalization and mortality, along with lower peak antibody titers and more rapid waning of immunity following vaccination in individuals with Down syndrome.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence of a distinct immune phenotype in Down syndrome that is driven by interferon hyperactivation, which has implications for understanding the mechanisms behind increased susceptibility to respiratory viral infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a framework for developing tailored preventive strategies and optimizing vaccination approaches in individuals with Down syndrome to address their heightened risk of severe respiratory viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on existing cohort data and does not present new experimental results, which limits the depth of mechanistic understanding. However, the integration of multiple lines of evidence supports a moderate confidence assessment.

流行病学 肯尼亚三级转诊中心住院儿童的病毒性和非典型细菌性呼吸道感染:一项横断面多重 PCR 研究

Epidemiology of viral and atypical bacterial respiratory infections among children hospitalised at a Kenyan tertiary referral centre: a cross sectional multiplex PCR study
作者： Rohini Kalagouda Patil, Anne Irungu, John Chelanga Supra, Rahul Zode, Koigi Paul Kamau, Joseph K Mbuthia, Diana Marangu-Boore
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 在撒哈拉以南非洲造成大量儿科发病率和 死亡率,需要当代病因学数据来指导预防策略。
设计与对象
对 2018 年 3 月至 2023 年 3 月期间因 SARI 住院且鼻咽拭子多重 qRT-PCR 检测呈阳性的肯尼亚 0-18 岁儿童进行了一项回顾性横断面分析研究。
核心方法
使用多重定量实时聚合酶链反应 (qRT-PCR) 检测 BioFire® 呼吸面板 2.0 (RP 2) 和 2.1 plus (RP2.1 plus) 来检测病原体;对病例的临床特征、放射学特征、实验室数据、季节性和严重感染的危险因素进行了分析。
主要结果
382 例病例中共检出 545 种病原体,其中检出多种微生物 123 例(22.5%),重症 14 例(4%),死亡 3 例(0.8%);鼻病毒(24%)和呼吸道合胞病毒(21%)是最常见的病毒病原体。
解释与创新
病毒占呼吸道感染的大部分,感染在第一个旱季和短暂的雨季达到高峰,这表明呼吸道 疫苗 的调度是季节性驱动的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 对肯尼亚儿科 SARI 住院造成了重大影响,疫苗 的时间应与季节性高峰保持一致,以最大限度地发挥影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对多重 PCR 的依赖(没有验证性病毒培养)限制了因果推断,并可能低估了合并感染,因此需要对证据有一定的信心。

Epidemiology of viral and atypical bacterial respiratory infections among children hospitalised at a Kenyan tertiary referral centre: a cross sectional multiplex PCR study

Authors: Rohini Kalagouda Patil, Anne Irungu, John Chelanga Supra, Rahul Zode, Koigi Paul Kamau, Joseph K Mbuthia, Diana Marangu-Boore
Original abstract (English)
Background Severe acute respiratory infections (SARIs) cause high paediatric morbidity and mortality particularly in sub-Saharan Africa. Understanding contemporary trends in SARI aetiology is needed to inform prevention strategies. We aimed to determine the current aetiology, severity and seasonality of SARIs based on pathogens detected by multiplex quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (qRT-PCR) tests, BioFire® respiratory panel 2.0 (RP 2) and 2.1 plus (RP2.1 plus ) Methods We conducted a retrospective cross-sectional analytic study among Kenyan children aged 0–18 years hospitalised between March 2018 and March 2023, with SARI and a positive nasopharyngeal swab multiplex qRT-PCR test. Cases were analyzed for their clinical characteristics, radiological features, laboratory data, seasonality of the respiratory pathogens and risk factors for severe infection. Results A total of 545 pathogens were detected in 382 cases,123 (22.5%) had polymicrobial detection, 14 (4%) had severe illness and three (0.8%) died. Rhinovirus (24%) and respiratory syncytial virus (21%) were the most common viral pathogens identified whereas Mycoplasma pneumoniae (2.4%) and Bordetella pertussis (1.8%) were the most common atypical pathogens detected. Infections peaked during the first dry season and the short rainy season. Lower respiratory tract infections and presence of co-morbidities like asthma,vitamin D deficiency and iron deficiency anaemia were associated with disease severity independent of age, sex and seasonality. Conclusions Viruses accounted for majority of respiratory infections. Infections had two distinct peaks: the first dry season and the short rainy season, suggesting that respiratory vaccines should be scheduled prior to their onset.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe acute respiratory infections (SARIs) impose substantial paediatric morbidity and mortality in sub-Saharan Africa, and contemporary aetiological data are needed to guide prevention strategies.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional analytic study was conducted among Kenyan children aged 0–18 years hospitalised with SARI and a positive nasopharyngeal swab multiplex qRT-PCR test between March 2018 and March 2023.
Core methods
Multiplex quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (qRT-PCR) tests, BioFire® respiratory panel 2.0 (RP 2) and 2.1 plus (RP2.1 plus), were used to detect pathogens; cases were analyzed for clinical characteristics, radiological features, laboratory data, seasonality and risk factors for severe infection.
Main results
A total of 545 pathogens were detected in 382 cases, with 123 (22.5%) polymicrobial detections, 14 (4%) severe illnesses and three (0.8%) deaths; rhinovirus (24%) and respiratory syncytial virus (21%) were the most common viral pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
Viruses accounted for the majority of respiratory infections, and infections peaked during the first dry season and the short rainy season, suggesting seasonality-driven scheduling of respiratory vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that RSV contributes substantially to paediatric SARI hospitalisations in Kenya and that vaccine timing should align with seasonal peaks to maximise impact.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on multiplex PCR without confirmatory viral culture limit causal inference and may underestimate co-infections, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

肺炎支原体肺炎婴儿喘息危险因素列线图的建立

Development of a nomogram for risk factors of wheezing in infants with Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia
作者： Chen Jianlong, Ye Yingying, Zhang Zhiling, Weifang Zhou, Wei Ji, Chu Chu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
构建肺炎支原体肺炎(MPP)婴儿喘息危险因素分析列线图模型,为临床干预提供依据。
设计与对象
回顾性分析苏州大学儿童医院呼吸内科和感染科住院的569例MPP婴儿的临床资料。根据症状分为喘息组(204例)和非喘息组(365例)。
核心方法
使用最小绝对收缩和选择算子 (LASSO) 回归进行初步筛查,然后使用 Logistic 回归确定 MPP 婴儿喘息的独立危险因素。建立列线图预测模型,并通过受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线和决策曲线分析(DCA)评估其性能。
主要结果
Multivariate Logistic regression showed that history of wheezing (OR = 5.894, 95%CI: 3.541–9.812), history of eczema (OR = 1.951, 95%CI: 1.295–2.939), elevated IgG (OR = 1.166, 95%CI: 1.074–1.266), elevated CKMB (OR = 1.083, 95%CI: 1.007–1.164), and combined HRSV infection (OR = 3.430, 95%CI: 1.819–6.468) were risk factors, while fever (OR = 0.389, 95%CI: 0.252–0.599) was a protective factor against wheezing in infants with MPP.
解释与创新
特应性体质和混合病毒感染是MPP婴儿喘息的危险因素。列线图模型对于预测 MPP 婴儿的喘息具有临床价值。
科研与公卫意义
列线图模型可以通过预测 MPP 婴儿的喘息为临床干预提供证据,有可能指导高危患者的早期识别和管理。
局限与证据强度
尽管校正后的 AUC 仍保持在 0.70 以上,但训练集 AUC (0.786) 的下降表明存在一定程度的过度拟合,这可能归因于单中心回顾性设计和有限的样本量。证据强度中等。

Development of a nomogram for risk factors of wheezing in infants with Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia

Authors: Chen Jianlong, Ye Yingying, Zhang Zhiling, Weifang Zhou, Wei Ji, Chu Chu
Original abstract (English)
To construct a nomogram model for analyzing risk factors of wheezing in infants with Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia (MPP), so as to provide evidence for clinical intervention. A retrospective analysis was performed on clinical data of 569 infants with MPP hospitalized in the Department of Respiratory Medicine and Department of Infectious Diseases, Children’s Hospital of Soochow University. They were divided into a wheezing group (204 cases) and a non-wheezing group (365 cases) according to symptoms. Demographic characteristics, clinical manifestations, laboratory examinations, bronchoscopy frequency and chest imaging findings were collected. Differences in clinical and laboratory indicators between the two groups were compared. Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (LASSO) regression was used for preliminary screening, followed by Logistic regression to identify independent risk factors for wheezing in infants with MPP. Finally, a nomogram prediction model was established, and its performance was evaluated by receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve and decision curve analysis (DCA). Univariate and multivariate analysis of predictors for wheezing in MPP: Univariate Logistic regression screening revealed 16 potential risk factors: fever, cough, history of wheezing, history of eczema, WBC, N#, L#, PLT, CRP, IgG, IgM, CKMB, D-D (ug/L), HRV, HRSV, HMPV ( P < 0.05). Multivariate Logistic regression showed that history of wheezing (OR = 5.894, 95%CI: 3.541–9.812), history of eczema (OR = 1.951, 95%CI: 1.295–2.939), elevated IgG (OR = 1.166, 95%CI: 1.074–1.266), elevated CKMB (OR = 1.083, 95%CI: 1.007–1.164), and combined HRSV infection (OR = 3.430, 95%CI: 1.819–6.468) were risk factors, while fever (OR = 0.389, 95%CI: 0.252–0.599) was a protective factor against wheezing in infants with MPP. Establishment and validation of the nomogram model: Based on multivariate Logistic regression, six indicators were included to construct the nomogram for predicting wheezing in MPP: history of wheezing, history of eczema, elevated IgG, elevated CKMB, combined HRSV infection, and fever. Each predictor was scored independently, and the total score corresponded to the predicted probability of wheezing caused by MPP. Model evaluation: Training set: AUC = 0.7862, sensitivity = 72.4%, specificity = 78.1%; Internal validation set: AUC = 0.6863, sensitivity = 53.7%, specificity = 79.5%, indicating favorable discrimination. DCA suggested potential clinical applicability.To further evaluate the robustness of the nomogram and to correct for potential optimism, we performed 200 bootstrap resampling iterations within the development cohort. The optimism-corrected area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) was calculated by subtracting the average optimism (estimated from the difference between apparent performance in each bootstrap sample and the performance applied to the original dataset) from the training-set AUC. The corrected AUC was 0.705 (95% CI: 0.662–0.748), which is consistent with the internal validation result (AUC = 0.686) and indicates moderate discriminative ability. Although the corrected AUC remained above 0.70, the decline from the training-set AUC (0.786) suggests some degree of overfitting, likely attributable to the single-center retrospective design and limited sample size. These bootstrap-corrected estimates support the potential clinical utility of the nomogram. Atopic constitution and mixed viral infection are risk factors for wheezing in infants with MPP. The nomogram model is clinically valuable for predicting wheezing in infants with MPP.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To construct a nomogram model for analyzing risk factors of wheezing in infants with Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia (MPP), so as to provide evidence for clinical intervention.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was performed on clinical data of 569 infants with MPP hospitalized in the Department of Respiratory Medicine and Department of Infectious Diseases, Children’s Hospital of Soochow University. They were divided into a wheezing group (204 cases) and a non-wheezing group (365 cases) according to symptoms.
Core methods
Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (LASSO) regression was used for preliminary screening, followed by Logistic regression to identify independent risk factors for wheezing in infants with MPP. A nomogram prediction model was established, and its performance was evaluated by receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve and decision curve analysis (DCA).
Main results
Multivariate Logistic regression showed that history of wheezing (OR = 5.894, 95%CI: 3.541–9.812), history of eczema (OR = 1.951, 95%CI: 1.295–2.939), elevated IgG (OR = 1.166, 95%CI: 1.074–1.266), elevated CKMB (OR = 1.083, 95%CI: 1.007–1.164), and combined HRSV infection (OR = 3.430, 95%CI: 1.819–6.468) were risk factors, while fever (OR = 0.389, 95%CI: 0.252–0.599) was a protective factor against wheezing in infants with MPP.
Interpretation and novelty
Atopic constitution and mixed viral infection are risk factors for wheezing in infants with MPP. The nomogram model is clinically valuable for predicting wheezing in infants with MPP.
Scientific and public-health significance
The nomogram model can provide evidence for clinical intervention by predicting wheezing in infants with MPP, potentially guiding early identification and management of high-risk patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although the corrected AUC remained above 0.70, the decline from the training-set AUC (0.786) suggests some degree of overfitting, likely attributable to the single-center retrospective design and limited sample size. The evidence strength is moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

成人 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染和社区获得性肺炎:发病机制、诊断挑战和临床意义。

Adult respiratory syncytial virus infection and community-acquired pneumonia: pathogenesis, diagnostic challenges and the clinical implications.
作者： Seong-Beom Park, Saeyong Lee, Sang-Kyu Shin, Jung Yeon Heo
摘要中文翻译简介:社区获得性肺炎 (CAP) 仍然是成人发病和 死亡率 的重要原因。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对成人 CAP 具有重要意义,特别是在老年人和患有慢性心肺疾病的患者中,但由于临床特征与其他呼吸道感染重叠且常规检测有限,因此经常被诊断不足。涵盖领域:本综述总结了 RSV 的关键病毒学和免疫学特征,强调了成人的诊断挑战,并讨论了呼吸道疾病以外的临床后果,包括心血管并发症、严重的院内结局以及抗生素暴露引起的继发细菌感染。利用韩国和国际数据,我们强调多样本分子检测在改善检测方面的价值,并讨论了 CAP 管理中抗菌药物管理的影响。我们还概述了 疫苗 在 3 期试验中的疗效以及在老年人中的早期现实世界疗效。专家意见:减少成人 RSV 负担需要在 RSV 循环期间建立可行的诊断途径,加强 监测 以捕获有临床意义的结果,并针对高危人群进行 疫苗接种。需要对免疫功能低下和其他高危人群进行持续的授权后评估和研究,以优化 疫苗接种 策略并为下一代预防提供信息。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染作为成人社区获得性肺炎 (CAP) 的原因,检查其 呼吸道合胞病毒、诊断挑战和临床意义。核心问题是由于临床特征重叠和常规检测有限,导致成人 CAP 中 RSV 诊断不足。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了韩国和国际数据,强调通过多样本分子检测来改进 RSV 检测。它没有指定数据库、搜索日期或研究计数,但强调了分子测试和现实世界 疫苗 有效性数据的使用。
共识与结论
RSV 是成人 CAP 的重要诱因,尤其是老年人和患有慢性心肺疾病的人。主要结论包括 RSV 与心血管并发症、严重院内结局和继发细菌感染的关联,以及 诊断 分子检测的价值。
争议与缺口
该审查指出了由于与其他呼吸道感染的临床重叠和有限的常规检测而导致的诊断挑战。证据差距包括需要对免疫功能低下和高危人群进行持续的授权后评估,以优化 疫苗接种 策略。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括在 RSV 循环期间开发可行的诊断途径、加强 监测 以获得具有临床意义的结果,以及针对高危人群针对 疫苗接种。需要进一步的研究来为下一代预防策略提供信息。

Adult respiratory syncytial virus infection and community-acquired pneumonia: pathogenesis, diagnostic challenges and the clinical implications.

Authors: Seong-Beom Park, Saeyong Lee, Sang-Kyu Shin, Jung Yeon Heo
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Community-acquired pneumonia (CAP) remains a significant cause of adult morbidity and mortality. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) contributes meaningfully to adult CAP, particularly in older adults and those with chronic cardiopulmonary disease, yet is frequently underdiagnosed because clinical features overlap with other respiratory infections and routine testing is limited. AREAS COVERED: This review summarizes key virological and immunological characteristics of RSV, highlights diagnostic challenges in adults, and discusses clinical consequences beyond respiratory illness, including cardiovascular complications, severe in-hospital outcomes, and secondary bacterial infection with antibiotic exposure. Using Korean and international data, we emphasize the value of multi-specimen molecular testing to improve detection and discuss the implications for antimicrobial stewardship in CAP management. We also provide an overview of vaccine efficacy in phase 3 trials and early real-world effectiveness in older adults. EXPERT OPINION: Reducing the adult RSV burden will require a feasible diagnostic pathway during periods of RSV circulation, strengthened surveillance that captures clinically meaningful outcomes, and targeted vaccination of high-risk populations. Ongoing post-authorization evaluation and research in immunocompromised and other high-risk groups are needed to optimize vaccination strategies and inform next-generation prevention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection as a cause of community-acquired pneumonia (CAP) in adults, examining its pathogenesis, diagnostic challenges, and clinical implications. The central question addresses the underdiagnosis of RSV in adult CAP due to overlapping clinical features and limited routine testing.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes Korean and international data, emphasizing multi-specimen molecular testing to improve RSV detection. It does not specify databases, search dates, or study counts but highlights the use of molecular testing and real-world vaccine effectiveness data.
Consensus and conclusions
RSV is a significant contributor to adult CAP, particularly in older adults and those with chronic cardiopulmonary disease. Key conclusions include the association of RSV with cardiovascular complications, severe in-hospital outcomes, and secondary bacterial infections, as well as the value of molecular testing for diagnosis.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes diagnostic challenges due to clinical overlap with other respiratory infections and limited routine testing. Evidence gaps include the need for ongoing post-authorization evaluation in immunocompromised and high-risk groups to optimize vaccination strategies.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include developing feasible diagnostic pathways during RSV circulation, strengthening surveillance for clinically meaningful outcomes, and targeting vaccination for high-risk populations. Further research is needed to inform next-generation prevention strategies.

COVID-19 后时代的 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 与日本老年人医疗信息源之间的关联:JACSIS 2024 年横断面研究。

Association between respiratory syncytial virus vaccination and medical information sources among Japanese older adults in the post-COVID-19 era: a JACSIS 2024 cross-sectional study.
作者： Masaya Tsutsumi, Kazuhiro Yamada, Kazuhisa Asai, Kentaro Ueno, Yuri Oshima, Takahiro Ueda, Kaho Hirai, Erika Toyokura, Yuichiro Furukawa, Atsushi Miyamoto
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了日本老年人对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 的吸收与他们的医疗信息来源、RSV 疫苗接种 资格知识以及后 COVID-19 时代的 疫苗接种 病史之间的关联。
证据基础与方法
该综述报告了一项在日本 60 岁或以上成年人中进行的全国性横断面在线调查,分析了 RSV 疫苗接种 状态与医疗信息来源、有关 RSV 感染和 疫苗接种 的知识以及既往 疫苗接种 病史之间的关联。
共识与结论
RSV vaccination uptake among older adults in Japan was low at 4.2%. Vaccination was positively associated with obtaining information from healthcare professionals (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 1.47, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.13-1.92), workplaces or schools (aOR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.05-1.87), academic websites (aOR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.05-2.20), LINE (aOR: 1.73, 95% CI: 1.21-2.48), and magazines (aOR: 1.62, 95% CI: 1.12-2.34).
争议与缺口
该综述没有报告亚组间的异质性、区域差异或 RSV 疫苗接种 吸收或信息源有效性的长期趋势。
科研与实践启示
来自医疗保健专业人员的建议可能支持个性化的 RSV 疫苗接种 推广,特别是在有 疫苗接种 病史的个人中,而 LINE 等基于推送的信息渠道可能有助于在人群层面传播 RSV 相关知识。

Association between respiratory syncytial virus vaccination and medical information sources among Japanese older adults in the post-COVID-19 era: a JACSIS 2024 cross-sectional study.

Authors: Masaya Tsutsumi, Kazuhiro Yamada, Kazuhisa Asai, Kentaro Ueno, Yuri Oshima, Takahiro Ueda, Kaho Hirai, Erika Toyokura, Yuichiro Furukawa, Atsushi Miyamoto
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes substantial morbidity among older adults; however, RSV vaccination remains limited in Japan. Identifying effective information channels to promote RSV vaccination is particularly important in the post-COVID-19 era. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide, cross-sectional online survey in Japan among adults aged ≥60 years. The analysis examined associations between RSV vaccination status and medical information sources, knowledge regarding RSV infection and vaccination, and prior vaccination history. RESULTS: Among the 8300 participants, RSV vaccination uptake was 4.2%. Individuals who received the RSV vaccine more frequently reported obtaining information from interpersonal or institution-based sources, including healthcare professionals (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 1.47, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.13-1.92) and workplaces or schools (aOR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.05-1.87), as well as from media-based sources such as academic websites (aOR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.05-2.20), LINE (aOR: 1.73, 95% CI: 1.21-2.48), and magazine (aOR: 1.62, 95% CI: 1.12-2.34). RSV vaccination was positively associated with having the knowledge that older adults are eligible for the RSV vaccination (aOR: 4.79, 95% CI: 3.45-6.67), as well as with prior vaccination against SARS-CoV-2 (aOR: 1.87, 95% CI: 1.38-2.53), influenza (aOR: 1.57, 95% CI: 1.21-2.03), and pneumococcal disease (aOR: 1.83, 95% CI: 1.44-2.33). CONCLUSIONS: RSV vaccination in older adults in Japan is associated with specific interpersonal and media-based sources of information. Recommendations from healthcare professionals may support individualized vaccination promotion, particularly among individuals with a prior vaccination history, whereas push-based information channels such as LINE may be useful for population-level dissemination of RSV-related knowledge.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the association between respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination uptake among older adults in Japan and their sources of medical information, knowledge about RSV vaccination eligibility, and prior vaccination history in the post-COVID-19 era.
Evidence base and method
The review reports a nationwide, cross-sectional online survey conducted among adults aged 60 years or older in Japan, analyzing associations between RSV vaccination status and medical information sources, knowledge regarding RSV infection and vaccination, and prior vaccination history.
Consensus and conclusions
RSV vaccination uptake among older adults in Japan was low at 4.2%. Vaccination was positively associated with obtaining information from healthcare professionals (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 1.47, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.13-1.92), workplaces or schools (aOR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.05-1.87), academic websites (aOR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.05-2.20), LINE (aOR: 1.73, 95% CI: 1.21-2.48), and magazines (aOR: 1.62, 95% CI: 1.12-2.34).
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report heterogeneity across subgroups, regional differences, or long-term trends in RSV vaccination uptake or information source effectiveness.
Research and practice implications
Recommendations from healthcare professionals may support individualized RSV vaccination promotion, particularly among individuals with prior vaccination history, while push-based information channels such as LINE may be useful for population-level dissemination of RSV-related knowledge.

帕利珠单抗用于卡塔尔高危人群的 呼吸道合胞病毒 免疫预防:呼吁采取行动。

Palivizumab for respiratory syncytial virus immunoprophylaxis in high-risk populations in Qatar: a call for action.
作者： Muna Al Maslamani, Bosco Paes, Mohamed Janahi, Eman Al Maslamani, Ahmed Abushahin, Ali Almudeer, Manasik Hassan, Moaaz Abo Zeed, Mai Alqubaisi, Ibrahim Janahi
摘要中文翻译简介:早产儿和高危婴儿发生严重 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染的风险增加,导致住院时间延长、入住重症监护病房、呼吸支持和死亡。尽管 RSV 预防是卡塔尔的一项主要卫生优先事项,但目前缺乏 RSV 预防的国家指南。涵盖领域:本专家意见总结了卡塔尔和海湾合作委员会 (GCC) 的 RSV 负担以及当前的免疫预防实践和帕利珠单抗对高危婴儿有效性的现实证据。卡塔尔和海湾合作委员会相关专家的见解补充了文献综述的证据,以解决 RSV 预防方面的数据差距和异质性。综合研究结果支持针对具体情况的建议。专家意见:由 16 名专家组成的指导委员会于 2024 年 12 月召开,审查现有证据并讨论高危婴儿 RSV 预防的挑战。专家们查阅了文献并就建议达成了共识。帕利珠单抗得到既定有效性和长期安全性数据的支持,仍然是早产儿和高危儿科人群 RSV 预防的基石。它还承认有必要与其他海湾合作委员会国家合作并制定区域协议,以进一步加强 RSV 预防工作。

范围与问题
本综述重点关注早产儿和高危婴儿的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染,检查卡塔尔和海湾合作委员会 (GCC) 的 RSV 负担,并评估当前的免疫预防实践和帕利珠单抗有效性的真实世界证据。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括文献综述,并辅以 2024 年 12 月召开的由 16 名专家组成的指导委员会的专家见解,以解决 RSV 预防中的数据差距和异质性。
共识与结论
帕利珠单抗被确立为早产儿和高危儿科人群 RSV 预防的基石,并得到有效性证据和长期安全性数据的支持。
争议与缺口
通过补充文献综述以及与卡塔尔和海湾合作委员会相关的专家见解,解决了 RSV 预防的证据差距和异质性。
科研与实践启示
制定了针对具体情况的建议,强调需要与其他海湾合作委员会国家合作,制定区域协议并加强 RSV 预防工作。

Palivizumab for respiratory syncytial virus immunoprophylaxis in high-risk populations in Qatar: a call for action.

Authors: Muna Al Maslamani, Bosco Paes, Mohamed Janahi, Eman Al Maslamani, Ahmed Abushahin, Ali Almudeer, Manasik Hassan, Moaaz Abo Zeed, Mai Alqubaisi, Ibrahim Janahi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Preterm and high-risk infants are at increased risk of severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, leading to prolonged hospitalization, intensive care unit admissions, respiratory support and death. Although, RSV prevention is a major health priority in Qatar, national guidelines for RSV prophylaxis are currently lacking. AREAS COVERED: This expert opinion summarizes the burden of RSV in Qatar and the Gulf Cooperation Council (GCC) along with current immunoprophylaxis practices and real-world evidence on the effectiveness of palivizumab in high-risk infants. Evidence from a literature review was supplemented by expert insights relevant to Qatar and the GCC to address data gaps and heterogeneity in RSV prophylaxis. The integrated findings support context-specific recommendations. EXPERT OPINION: A steering committee with 16 experts was convened in December 2024 to review the available evidence and discuss challenges in RSV prevention among high-risk infants. The experts reviewed the literature and reached consensus for the recommendations. Palivizumab, supported by established effectiveness and long-term safety data, remains the cornerstone of RSV prophylaxis for preterm and high-risk pediatric population. It also acknowledges the necessity to collaborate with other GCC countries and develop a regional protocol to further strengthen the RSV prevention efforts.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in preterm and high-risk infants, examining the burden of RSV in Qatar and the Gulf Cooperation Council (GCC) and evaluating current immunoprophylaxis practices and real-world evidence on palivizumab effectiveness.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes a literature review supplemented by expert insights from a steering committee of 16 experts convened in December 2024 to address data gaps and heterogeneity in RSV prophylaxis.
Consensus and conclusions
Palivizumab is established as the cornerstone of RSV prophylaxis for preterm and high-risk pediatric populations, supported by evidence of effectiveness and long-term safety data.
Controversies and gaps
Evidence gaps and heterogeneity in RSV prophylaxis were addressed by supplementing literature review with expert insights relevant to Qatar and the GCC.
Research and practice implications
Context-specific recommendations were developed, emphasizing the need to collaborate with other GCC countries to establish a regional protocol and strengthen RSV prevention efforts.

儿科医生对 呼吸道合胞病毒 以及当前和新兴预防策略的认知、知识和态度:来自土耳其一家三级护理医院的横断面调查研究

Awareness, knowledge, and attitudes of pediatricians towards respiratory syncytial virus and current and emerging prophylactic strategies: a cross-sectional survey study from a tertiary care hospital in Turkey
作者： Naci Yılmaz, Aslınur Özkaya Parlakay, Metin Yigit, Kaan Celebier, Furkan Kalaycı, Orkun Tolunay, Naci YILMAZ, Metin Yiğit, Kaan Çelebier
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染是五岁以下儿童下呼吸道感染的最常见原因,也是导致住院的重要因素。本研究旨在调查儿科医生(包括儿科专家和儿科住院医生)对 RSV 感染以及当地可用的(帕利珠单抗)和新出现的全球预防方案(尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 疫苗接种)的知识、意识和态度。对于这项横断面调查研究,数据是通过 2024 年 6 月至 2024 年 10 月期间对 216 名儿科医生(包括 76 名儿科专家和 140 名儿科住院医生)进行的在线调查收集的。几乎所有参与者 (97.7%) 都意识到应对高危人群的儿童进行 RSV 预防,以减少 RSV 感染对住院、发病率和 死亡率 的影响。然而,只有 78.7% 的参与者了解 疫苗接种 和用于预防 RSV 的单克隆抗体 (mAb)。共有 165 名 (76.4%) 参与者建议专门针对高危婴儿进行预防。还评估了对母亲 疫苗接种 的认识。相比之下,那些没有接种疫苗的人大多不知道这种疾病的发生频率和成本,并认为仅母乳就足以预防这种疾病。大约 55% 的儿科医生认为 RSV 预防措施没有得到有效实施。大约 83% 的参与者表示,如果他们有孩子,他们会选择 RSV 预防,并相信 RSV 预防可以有效影响许多人,从而减轻感染负担。共有 113 名参与者 (52.3%) 认为单克隆抗体应该给所有婴儿注射。值得注意的是,儿科专家对预防方法的认识明显高于儿科住院医生 (p = 0.004),而且临床症状和 传播 途径也存在差异。我们的研究结果表明,提高儿科医生对 RSV 诊断、治疗 和预防的了解对于优化预防策略至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染(五岁以下儿童下呼吸道感染的最常见原因),并调查儿科医生对 RSV 预防的知识、意识和态度,包括当地可用的和新兴的选择。
证据基础与方法
这是一项横断面调查研究,通过 2024 年 6 月至 2024 年 10 月期间对 216 名儿科医生(76 名专家和 140 名住院医生)进行的在线调查进行。
共识与结论
几乎所有参与者 (97.7%) 都同意应对高危婴儿进行 RSV 预防,以减少住院、发病率和 死亡率。儿科专家对预防方法的认识明显高于住院医师 (p = 0.004)。
争议与缺口
只有 78.7% 的参与者了解 疫苗接种 和用于预防 RSV 的单克隆抗体。大约 55% 的人认为预防措施没有得到有效实施,未接种疫苗的参与者往往不知道疾病的发生频率和费用。
